{D4345}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_sha li ho tra ya yAM a shwa A yur bai d+ya saM hi ta nAm/_bod skad du/_rta'i tshe'i rig byed sha li ho tras bsdus ba zhes bya ba/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas sman gyi bla 
[\[156b.2\]]baiDUr+ya 'od kyi rgyal po la phyag 'tshal lo/_/khyab 'jug gi ni btsun mo'i mchog_/lha mo dpal mo rta yi mngal/_/phyag 'tshal nas ni rta rnams kyi/_/tshe yi rig byed rtogs bshad bya/_/gang las gang phyir gang don yin/_/de dag kun kyang brjod par bya/_/kha ba can gyi 
[\[156b.3\]]byang phyogs na/_/lha yi dbang po'i gnas 'dra bar/_/sha li ho tra drang srong che/_/sbyin sreg byas nas 'dug pa'i tshe/_/legs thos thos pa phun tshogs ldan/_/bu yis pha la dris gyur pa/_/legs ldan chos rnams thams cad mkhyen/_/rta tshe'i rig byed mkhas 
[\[156b.4\]]lags na/_/rta rnams kyi ni gso ba 'di/_/ji ltar byung bar gyur pa lags/_/drang srong gzi brjid chen po yis/_/de la mnyan par gyis zhes smras/_/sngon tshe rta rnams rang dgar ni/_/mkha' la rgyu zhing gnas byed cing /_/zas dang spyod pa ci dga' bar/_/de rnams rang 
[\[156b.5\]]dgar spyod pa yin/_/thams cad la yang lus kyi ni/_/goms pas nad ni byung ma gyur/_/gang nas brtsams te nga yi bu/_/bzhon pa nyid du byas gyur pa/_/de nas brtsams te gnod byed pa'i/_/nad kun gyis ni gzir pa yin/_/de nas de la sems gdung skyes/_/nga 
[\[156b.6\]]yis dka' thub chen po byas/_/tshangs pa lha ni mnyes byas nas/_/de yi gso ba rnyed pa yin/_/zug rngu lus kyi gso ba dang /_/de bzhin cha spyad nyid dang ni/_/rte'u yi ni gso ba dang /_/dug gi rang bzhin sman dang ni/_/bcud len gdon nad gso ba dang /_/ro tsa byed pa

[\[161a.1\]]rnams rdzogs par byed nus pas/_/las rdzogs zhes ni tshangs pas bshad/_/mgyogs pa dang ni mgyogs 'gro dang /_/de bzhin myur 'gro can zhes pa/_/de ni mgyogs pa kho nar bshad/_/'di dag nges tshig tshal yin no/_/rta ni dpal mo'i 
[\[161a.2\]]bu yin pas/_/dpal mo'i bu zhes bshad pa yin/_/mtshan dang rigs ni bzang ba dang /_/dpa' pos bzhon zhing byang ba yi/_/rta ni stong gis sa bzhi dag_/ma lus pa las rgyal bar nus/_/mtshon thogs pa yis mi thub pa'i/_/gnas der stobs che ba yi rtas/_/'phral 
[\[161a.3\]]du rgyal bar nus pa yin/_/de phyir khyed kyis de la ni/_/shin tu gus shing brtse bar bya/_/rgyal po sman pa'i chos don dang /_/phun tshogs pa yang gzhan las min/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las nges pa'i tshig gi le'u ste gsum pa'o//_//sha li ho tra 
[\[161a.4\]]blo gros che/_/rta yi gnas na 'dug pa la/_/legs thos thos pa phun tshogs pas/_/slar yang pha la dris gyur pa/_/rta ni ci ltar byung bar gyur/_/mchod sbyin 'tsham pa ci ltar 'gyur/_/de ni dang por rgyal po dag_/gang gi bzhon pa nyid du 'gyur/_/de yi rigs bzhi ci lta bu/_/
[\[161a.5\]]de lta bur ni ci ltar 'gyur/_/ci yi phyir ni de 'drar 'gyur/_/de dag shes par 'tshal lags so/_/de yi tshig ni thos pa dang /_/sha li ho tras smras gyur pa/_/'di na rgyal po'i chos kyi mchog_/sa rnams yongs su skyong ba yin/_/de yang dpung gis byed par nus/_/dpung 
[\[161a.6\]]gi mchog ni rta rnams yin/_/de phyir rang don la mkhas pa'i/_/rgyal po'i chos don 'dod don du/_/yang ye rnam pa thams cad du/_/'bad de rta rnams bsrung bar bya/_/chos dang grags 'dod sman pa ni/_/bstan bcos mkhas gyur pas kyang bsten/_/de bas de 
[\[161a.7\]]rnams lha dang ni/_/mi ni ji ltar byung ba bshad/_/rigs dang nye ba'i rigs dang tshe/_/na tshod shis dang mi shis dang /_/gtsug dang gdong dang thig le dang /_/'tsher dang 'gros kyi rim pa dang /_/mtshan ngan gnyen po mchan khung dang /_/'byung dang bzhon 
[\[161b\]]
[\[161b.1\]]ba'i bya ba dang /_/nad med nad ni rgyas pa dang /_/rna ba rnams kyi sman dang ni/_/de bzhin sman ni byung ba yi/_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]ci bzhin nyid dang ni/_/spu mdog mdog 'dres khyad par dang /_/spu mdog dag dang mig dag dang /_/rang bzhin snying stobs dag dang ni/_/ston ka 
[\[161b.2\]]skyes pa'i mtshan nyid de/_/'di dag thams cad mdor bsdus nas/_/khyed la legs par bshad par bya/_/lha rnams kyis ni mngal skyes dang /_/mngal med byung ba gnyis su bshad/_/sngon tshe 'jig rten zhig gyur cing /_/'jig rten 'di ni med pa'i tshe/_/'byin byed 
[\[161b.3\]]me la sbyin sreg ni/_/byas pas dang por tshangs pa dang /_/rta pho de rjes rgod ma mchog_/rang gi skye gnas dag tu bskyed/_/gzhan yang skye dgu'i bdag mo yi/_/mchod sbyin la ni tshangs pa yi/_/mchi ma byung ba dpal mo ni/_/dga' bas snyim pa sbyar bas blangs/_/de 
[\[161b.4\]]yi yan lag las byung ste/_/rta rnams bram ze'i rigs sogs gyur/_/kha dang dpung pa brla gnyis dang /_/rkang pa gnyis las [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/g.yas g.yon las ni pho mo ste/_/tshangs pa las rta de ltar skyes/_/'o ma'i rgya mtsho bsrubs pa'i tshe/_/rta dang bdud rtsi lhan 
[\[161b.5\]]cig byung /_/de ming u tsa sra ba ste/_/sgra ni sprin chen lta bu yin/_/rgya mtsho de las nam mkha' la/_/shugs kyis rnam par 'gro ba na/_/lha min stobs dang lha 'khrug pas/_/phyi rkang gnyis ni bzung bar gyur/_/stobs chen stobs ldan gyis drangs pas/_/dpyi ni cung zad 
[\[161b.6\]]nyams par gyur/_/de bas rta rnams da rung mi/_/dpyi ni myur bar mi 'debs shing /_/ngal so ba na rkang pa ni/_/phyi ma res kyis dal bar 'degs/_/tshangs pa'i bur gyur gtum po ni/_/dmod pa yis ni 'ug par gyur/_/yid 'ong yid kyi mchod sbyin ni/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]mor gyur de 
[\[161b.7\]]gnyis 'dus/_/de la sgo nga sna tshogs pa/_/du ma byung bar gyur ba yin/_/lte ba man chad mes gdungs pa'i/_/rdul ni thal bar 'dzag gyur pa/_/de las kyang ni kha dog rnams/_/tha dad bong pho bong mor gyur/_/sgra skad rtsub cing rno ba dang /_/rang bzhin rtsub cing rkang pa rtsub/_/de yang 
[\[162a\]]
[\[162a.1\]]rta dang lhan cig byung /_/skye dgu'i bdag gi gnas na 'dug_/de nas de dag rmongs pa yis/_/de yi sbyin sreg khang pa brlag_/des ni de la bsad dang bcings/_/sgro bcad rtswa ni za ba dang /_/lha dang mi yi bzhon pa ni/_/nyid gyur cig ces dmod pa 
[\[162a.2\]]btab/_/rta de yid ni khums gyur nas/_/skye dgu'i bdag la gsol ba btab/_/de nas de la mgu gyur nas/_/mchog 'di thob par gyur ba yin/_/rta yi mchod sbyin la bsad dang /_/g.yul du g.yo bar gyur pa dang /_/legs par sbyin pa btang pa na/_/bsod nams khyed kyis 
[\[162a.3\]]thob gyur cig_/de bzhin du ni yon gnas kyi/_/mchog nyid du ni rta gyur cig_/dad pas dge sbyong bram ze la/_/rta rnams byin par gyur na ni/_/rta yi ba spu ji snyed par/_/lha yul du ni skye bar 'gyur/_/sbyin pa btang pa'i rta yang ni/_/'gro ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ba yin/_/de nas 
[\[162a.4\]]skye dgu'i bdag po yis/_/tshig gis 'dus nas kha ba can/_/sha li ho tra la 'khur zhing /_/drang srong rnams dang lhan cig rtse/_/stobs ldan u tsa sra ba ni/_/zhon nas lha rnams gzir bar byed/_/lha kun mngon mtho 'dod pa yis/_/sha li ho tra la gsol btab/_/de nas 
[\[162a.5\]]lha la bzhon pa'i phyir/_/ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du byin/_/zla ba la dkar lha chen la/_/ljang gu khyab 'jug la rta ser/_/marka ta mdog 'dra nyi ma la/_/me lha la dmar gshin rje nag_/pad+ma dkar 'dra chu lha la/_/sngo dang khra bo tha skar gyi/_/bu gnyis dmar po rlung lha la/_/lha 
[\[162a.6\]]rnams gzhan la sna tshogs pa/_/rang rang ji ltar dga' ba ni/_/'di 'dra yid 'phrog byed pa byin/_/lag ma'i rta ni skye dgu'i bdag_/yid ches byas pas blangs nas ni/_/de yis mi yi rgyal po la/_/ci rigs bar ni bgos te byin/_/gshog pa bcad pas de rnams ni/_/
[\[162a.7\]]na gyur slar yang me yis bsregs/_/nad gdungs sha li ho tra la/_/'khus par gyur te khro ba yi/_/mche ba bcad nas stobs kyis ni/_/de gnyid log pa bza' snyam na/_/de tshe drang srong mi snang gyur/_/de nas rta rnams zos snyam nas/_/dga' bas rta spangs 'byin cing snom/_/
[\[162b\]]
[\[162b.1\]]de phyir rta rnams da rung yang /_/rta spangs btang nas snom par byed/_/skye dgu'i bdag gi khyim bdag tshe/_/ha ha zer bas ha yar bshad/_/bya ltar gshog pa mgyogs 'gro bas/_/mgyogs 'gro zhes ni bshad pa yin/_/lam rdzogs mgyogs pas byed pa ni/_/'phrog 
[\[162b.2\]]pa 'tsher ba nyid la'ang bya/_/lha rnams kyis ni rta byung ba/_/rnam pa bzhi ste du ma'ang yin/_/bong bu rgod ma la dre'u/_/bong bu bas ni bzang bar 'dod/_/dre'u las skyes ki na yin/_/de las skyes pa ki sa ta ta/_/ki sa ta ta'i bu chu bur can/_/chu bur can bu bong bur gyur/_/
[\[162b.3\]]rang dang rta yi skye gnas te/_/bong bu'i skye gnas gnyis su 'dod/_/bong bu dang ni rta sogs la/_/lugs su 'byung dang lugs las bzlog_/de ltar rigs kyi dbye ba 'di/_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du brjod pa yin/_/rta rnams kyi ni 'byung ba dag_/slar yang 'od chen bka' stsol cig_/
[\[162b.4\]]'o ma'i rgya mtsho las byung nyid/_/thams cad shes par 'tshal lags so/_/bu yis de ltar dris pa dang /_/sha li ho tras smras pa ni/_/skye dgu dang po byung ba'i tshe/_/'tsho ba'i 'jig rten bde gnas gyur/_/de rjes lha dang lha min gyi/_/'jig rten kun la tshangs pa yis/_/'jig rten 
[\[162b.5\]]thams cad khyab byed pa'i/_/skom gyis gdungs pa nyid du byas/_/'jig rten pa yi srog chags rnams/_/slar yang rgyug cing 'gro bar byed/_/lha dang lha min sha za dang /_/lto 'phye srin po de rnams kyang /_/stobs dang ldan pa'i skom gyis ni/_/gdungs bas nye bar zin par gyur/_/lha 
[\[162b.6\]]dang lha min kha zas dang /_/mal stan la ni dga' med gyur/_/de rnams de ltar sems pa na/_/de yi tshe na blo skyes pa/_/gang 'di chu yi snying por ni/_/gnas der bdag cag skom sel gyur/_/de nas ri bo man+dAr/_/grags ldan srub shing nyid du byas/_/klu yi rgyal chen mtha' 
[\[162b.7\]]yas ni/_/srub zag tu ni yang dag sbyar/_/der ni phyogs gcig lha rnams te/_/phyogs gcig tu ni lha min gnas/_/srub thag 'dren zhing mtsho chen ni/_/'jigs pa'i sgra rnams bsrubs pa na/_/mtsho chen der ni lha dang mi'i/_/sman ni rtsa ba lo ma dang /_/'o mar bcas pa bsdus nas ni/_/
[\[163a\]]
[\[163a.1\]]thams cad bcug pa nyid du 'gyur/_/gzi brjid che zhing gzi brjid can/_/ro la sogs pa 'byung zhing dge_/me tog ser dang go sring gi_/de bzhin sor mo la spyod dang /_/mdangs ldan dzA ti sa ra dang /_/'tsho byed 'chi ba gso byed dang /_/de bzhin rma 'tsho byed la 
[\[163a.2\]]sogs/_/gzhan yang tshangs bas gang sprul ba'i/_/bcu dang mthu stobs bsam mi khyab/_/sman rnams mtsho chen der bcug ste/_/rgya mtsho bsrubs par gyur pa na/_/de tshe dang po kho nar ni/_/dpal mo la sogs byung par 'gyur/_/gang gis nang par brjod na ni/_/rmi ngan 
[\[163a.3\]]la sogs 'jig byed pa'i/_/dge ba zhes bya'i rin po che/_/brgyad ni chu las byung bar gyur/_/nyi ma bzhon pa'i shing rta ni/_/'dren pa'i rta dang lha yi chang /_/de bzhin nor bzang ko thub/_/nor bu'i rna cha bzang po dang /_/bdud rtsi zhes bya'i sman dang ni/_/bdud rtsi nyid kyang byung bar 
[\[163a.4\]]'gyur/_/bstan bcos thams cad 'dzin pa'i mchog_/sman pa thang la 'bar dang ni/_/sgra mtho zhes ni bya ba rta/_/snying stobs che zhing stobs che la/_/'brug dang rnga yi sgra dang ni/_/dung dang rnga chen sgra 'dra ba/_/gang zhig skad gsang mthon po yis/_/sgra des ming du btags pa ni/_/

[\[163a.6\]]las bzung /_/de tshe lha min thams cad kyis/_/man+dAr dang de bzhin du/_/klu yi rgyal po bor nas ni/_/rta yi rin chen la 'jus te/_/'o ma'i rgya mtsho spangs nas ni/_/sa gzhi 'dir ni phyin par gyur/_/lha min bum pa mnyam pa dang /_/de bzhin sems rab 'phags pa dang /_/
[\[163a.7\]]me 'dra ba dang 'dod lha dang /_/gnod skyobs gser mdog ces bya ba/_/chu bur zlum po zhes bya bas/_/stobs ldan la ni tshig 'di smras/_/gang gis dpal thob 'gyur pa yi/_/don gnyer rin chen thob pas na/_/bdag cag lha dang mi rnams la/_/'jigs ba 'byung bar mi 'gyur ro/_/
[\[163b\]]
[\[163b.1\]]rtag par rab tu dga' byed cing /_/thams cad stobs kyis dregs pa dang /_/lha min skyes bu thams cad ni/_/dga' zhing mgu ba nyid du bya/_/de nas stobs ldan yid dga' bas/_/des ni lha rnams la smras pa/_/rgya mtsho chen po bsrubs pa las/_/rin chen gang zhig thob gyur pa/_/de 
[\[163b.2\]]ni ba la byin pas na/_/gzhan gyis de rnyed 'on ma yin/_/lha rnams kyis kyang tshig smras pa/_/bdag cag gi yang rtsal yin pas/_/rin chen khyod ni gcig bu'i min/_/khyed dang bdag cag thun mong yin/_/de nas lo ni khri yi bar/_/skar ma'i tshad kyis g.yul 'byed de/_/lha dang lha min 
[\[163b.3\]]g.yul ngor ni/_/dpung tshogs ldang zhing ldang zhing 'thab/_/de tshe stobs ldan sgra mtho ba'i/_/stobs mgyogs ldan pa'i rta zhon nas/_/lha rnams kyi ni dpung gi tshogs/_/chen po dag kyang brlags par gyur/_/gang yang der ni lha rnams kyis/_/g.yul du bsad pa'i lha min rnams/_/
[\[163b.4\]]rta dbang de yis bsnams pa dang /_/yang dang yang du slar 'tsho gyur/_/de nas lha min chen po yis/_/bsad par gyur pas rmongs nas ni/_/skyabs 'dod pa yis tshangs pa la/_/skyabs su nye bar song bar gyur/_/legs ldan stobs chen ldan pa yi/_/lha min g.yul gyis pham par 
[\[163b.5\]]bgyis/_/legs ldan gyis ni bdag cag la/_/bzhon pa bzang po stsal ba'i rigs/_/bdag cag gis kyang lha min gyi/_/g.yul ngo'i gnas las rgyal bar bgyi/_/de nas tshangs pa skye dgu yi/_/bdag pos de la smras pa ni/_/phyis 'byung ba ni gang yang rung /_/nga nyid kyis ni sngar shes 
[\[163b.6\]]te/_/gang tshe gang gis ci tsam 'gyur/_/'di ni kho bos sngar shes pas/_/nga yis sngon tshe rta sprul bas/_/deb ni nas steng 'gro ba dang /_/bar snang dang ni sa 'og gi_/rgyu dang mi rgyu gang yin dang /_/srog chags rnams ni gang ci'ang rung /_/de dag thams cad mngon sprul to/_/mchod 
[\[163b.7\]]sbyin bzhon pa'i don du ni/_/rta yang nga yis sngon phyung ste/_/sbyin sreg byed tshe nga yi ni/_/mchi ma 'dzag par gyur pa de/_/dpal mo yid ni dga' ba yis/_/lag pa gnyis kyis blangs par gyur/_/de las der ni rta byung bas/_/de phyir dpal mo'i bu zhes bya/_/der ni lag pa 
[\[164a\]]
[\[164a.1\]]g.yas pa yi/_/mchi ma las ni rta phor gyur/_/g.yon pa'i mchi ma las rgod ma/_/snying stobs ldan dang mgyogs mdangs ldan/_/dpal mo'i lag pa'i nga'i mchi ma/_/der ni rta de bram ze'i rigs/_/nga yi mchi ma dpal mo yi/_/lag pa gnyis las 
[\[164a.2\]]'dzag gyur pa/_/brang na gnas pa rgyal rigs te/_/g.yul ngor sra bar shes par bya/_/lte ba brla yi rje'u rigs te/_/rkang pa 'dzag pa dmangs rigs yin/_/der ni g.yon ngos rgod ma ste/_/g.yas ngos kyis ni rta por bshad/_/'di rnams phan tshun 'dus pa ni/_/rigs bzhi dag tu bshad 
[\[164a.3\]]pa yin/_/de ltar rta rnams yang dag byung /_/yid ltar mgyogs zhing mdog ldan te/_/mdog gcig mdog ni 'dres pa dang /_/sna tshogs mdangs kyis phye ba dang /_/thig le can dang kha dog mnga'/_/mtshan ma can dang zhur pa dang /_/rkang dkar rdog ma mun ja 'dra/_/rgyal mtshan 
[\[164a.4\]]dkyil 'khor 'dra ba dang /_/rnga rngog mdog ni mang ba dang /_/khra bo thig le phra ba dang /_/'dams mdog se ba nyid dang ni/_/dkar la rngog ma gnag pa ste/_/phal cher kha dog sna tshogs shing /_/ba spu snum zhing bzang ba dang /_/tshad dang ldan zhing mig bzang la/_/ro stod mtho bar gyur 
[\[164a.5\]]ba dang /_/dbus phra gsus khyim chung ba dang /_/mchu legs brang zheng yangs pa dang /_/rngog ma rked pa rmig pa bzang /_/pags srab spu mtshams phra ba dang /_/yid du 'ong zhing phum phum bzang /_/snying stobs ldan zhing rkang mdog mthun/_/lus las skyes pa'i gshog pa bzhi/_/nam 
[\[164a.6\]]mkha' 'gebs pa lta bu dang /_/yan lag dang ni nying lag rnams/_/lha yi char ldan gzi brjid che/_/dpal ldan byung ba dpal ma'i bu/_/tshangs pa'i mchi ma las byung mgyogs/_/me yis gdungs pa las gyur pa'i/_/lte ba las mi byung ba'i rdul/_/thal bar 'dzag gyur de las 
[\[164a.7\]]ni/_/bong bu dang ni bong mor gyur/_/rna ba ring zhing mjug mar ldan/_/gzhung thig phra zhing rmeg pa sra/_/skad gdangs rtsub cing khur sran che/_/gang zhig rtsub pa bong bu yin/_/thal ba las ni skyes pa'i phyir/_/de bas bong bu thal mdog ldan/_/de dag de ltar gyur nas ni/_/skye 
[\[164b\]]
[\[164b.1\]]dgu'i bdag mo'i gnas na 'dug_/de nas dregs pas rtse ba dang /_/sreg rdzas dgang gzar blugs gzar dang /_/gzhong pa snod dang bum pa bsnams/_/rmongs ba'i rta yis rab tu brlag_/'jag mag thal bas khyab pa yi/_/mi gang de ni mthong ba dang /_/dang khra khros par 
[\[164b.2\]]gyur nas na/_/rta rnams dmod pa btab par gyur/_/gshog bcad mtshan ni nub pa dang /_/sa la rgyu zhing tshig mi 'don/_/lha rnams dang ni mi rnams kyi/_/bzhon pa nyid du song gyur cig_/de thos sdug bsngal gyis gdungs te/_/de tshe rta rnams dang khra ni/_/de nyid la ni 
[\[164b.3\]]skyabs song nas/_/slar yang rjes su phan btags byas/_/rta yi mchod sbyin 'bras bu ni/_/bsod nams don du 'gro gyur cing /_/mchod pa bzang po thob gyur cing /_/bsod nams 'jig rten gyis thob shog_/phan btags thob pas de dga' ste/_/dang khra la ni bskor bar 
[\[164b.4\]]byas/_/mgo bos phyag byas bdag cag gis/_/ci zhig bgyis zhes tshig tu smras/_/dang khras der ni lung bsgo ba/_/kha ba can du khyed song la/_/ri 'dab yid du 'ong ba dang /_/chu dang ldan par gnas par gyis/_/de skad smras nas rta rnams ni/_/dga' ba ri rgyal dag tu song /_/
[\[164b.5\]]sbyin sreg bshigs pas khros pa na/_/dang khras ha ha zhes bzlog pas/_/de phyir 'di la ha yi sgra/_/rta rnams la ni btags pa yin/_/ri bo dam pa de ru ni/_/rta rnams gnas par gyur bas ni/_/gang zhig 'dod pa spyod gyur pa/_/rigs ni 'dres par gyur pa'i rgyus/_/rta dang bong 
[\[164b.6\]]bu de dag gi_/skye gnas 'dre bar bshad par bya/_/bong bu rta la 'jug gyur pa/_/gang yin de las dre'u 'byung /_/dre'u dang rgod ma dag las ni/_/go ri zhes ni bya ba skye/_/go ri rgod mar 'jug pa las/_/ki ni zhes ni bya ba skye/_/ki ni rgod ma las skyes pa/_/kis ta ta zhes bya 
[\[164b.7\]]ba yin/_/ki sa ta ta dang rgod ma las/_/chu bur can zhes bya ba yin/_/chu bur can dang rgod ma las/_/slar yang bong bu zhes byar bshad/_/bong bu 'ong bar mthong na ni/_/rta ni thams cad rmongs par 'gyur/_/bya ba mang zhing bslab par dka'/_/de phyir bong bu bzhon 
[\[165a\]]
[\[165a.1\]]dgra yin/_/de ltar 'di ni dre'u ste/_/ma 'dres 'jug las rta nyid yin/_/rang dang rta yi skye gnas te/_/bong bu'i skye gnas gnyis su 'dod/_/rta rnams gzhan gyi mngal du ni/_/'khrig pa spyod par mi byed la/_/gal te byed par gyur na ni/_/yul de 'jig par 
[\[165a.2\]]shes par bya/_/de ltar nga ni rta 'byin la/_/mchod sbyin don ni phyugs kyi mchog_/dgra 'joms rgyas byed la sogs pa/_/rgyal po rnams kyi dpung gi mchog_/de nas kha don la dga' zhing /_/sbyin sreg byed la rab gzhol ba/_/gzhon nu tshangs par spyod la gnas/_/sha li ho tra 
[\[165a.3\]]dka' thub che/_/kha ba can gyi ngos kyi nags/_/lha yi tshal 'drar 'ongs par gyur/_/de ni de ru gnas bcas nas/_/ri yi tshogs ni nyams dga' zhing /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]tshogs ni du mas khyab/_/mi'am ci ni sna tshogs gnas/_/bya tshogs du ma sgra sgrogs shing /_/dri za'i bu mo rnams kyis bskor/_/
[\[165a.4\]]grub dang drang srong rol ba yi/_/tshogs dang lha rnams dag gis mdzes/_/me tog 'bras bu mang po yis/_/nyams dga' ne'u gseng bzang pos khyab/_/shin tu yid du 'ong ba'i gnas/_/nyams dga' der ni gnas par gyur/_/der ni phye leb yan chad kyi/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]
[\[165a.5\]]la sogs sna tshogs gnas/_/rta dang dri za mi'am ci/_/ne'u le sbrul dang bya rnams ni/_/seng ge stag dang pag dang ni/_/glang po'i dbang po spre'u dang dom/_/de la sogs pa mang po ni/_/sha li ho tra drang srong che/_/bdag nyid thul cing gtsang ba yis/_/gnas dang nye bar

[\[170a\]]
[\[170a.1\]]de ltar na ni btsas par bshad/_/de yi rjes la sha ma ni/_/mngal nas 'byung bar 'gyur ba yin/_/pho la sogs pa mngal du ni/_/chags par byed pa'i rgyu nyon cig_/khu ba mang zhing khrag nyung pa/_/rlung gis 'dres par byas nas ni/_/der ni

[\[170a.3\]]dus su ni/_/pha ma dag kyang nyam chung zhing /_/dbang po'i stobs kyang zad gyur pa/_/de gnyis phrad par gyur pa ni/_/khu ba khrag kyang cha mnyam na/_/ma ning du ni skye bar 'gyur/_/nyes pa de dag gnyis dang ni/_/pha ma nad gzhir bcas gyur na/_/zla mtshan nyes pa cher

[\[170a.6\]]'gyur/_/de las gzhan yang mngal gyi ni/_/nyes pa bzhi bo mnyan par gyis/_/rlung nad gnod kyi nyes pa yis/_/mngal du zhu bar gyur pa yin/_/de la bad kan lhags gyur na/_/mngal na nyal ba zhes byar 'gyur/_/rlung dang bad kan gyis nyen pas/_/mngal 
[\[170a.7\]]du lugs pa zhes byar 'gyur/_/rgod ma rtseg cing me drod nyams/_/mngal skam pa zhes bya bar 'gyur/_/mngal du rlung gis gzir gyur cing /_/'jug tshe shin tu gnod byas la/_/bad kan khrag dang 'dres pa las/_/de tshe mtshan mi ldang bar 'gyur/_/sbrum

[\[171a.1\]]pa'am/_/rngog ma srab par 'gyur ba yin/_/pags pa spu dang rnga ma yi/_/skyon gang yod de rlung las gyur/_/bad kan mkhris pa las gnas pa'i/_/so yi rtsa bar gnas gyur la/_/bad kan mkhris pa shas che las/_/so mi sdug par 'gyur pa

[\[172a.1\]]thos thos dang ldan pa yis/_/tshul bzhin yongs su dris ba na/_/sha li ho tra dka' thub ches/_/dris lan de ltar smras pa yin/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las mngal du chags pa zhes bya ba ste le'u lnga pa'o//_//rta skad las

[\[179b.1\]]do ste/_/de rjes rnga rtsa yin par bshad/_/de yi ngos gnyis dbyi steng yin/_/dpyi steng 'og ni ri hi ni/_/bar na rnga ma rnga snying bcas/_/de gnyis 'og ni brla dpyi'i mtshams/_/rnga rtsa'i 'og ni rkub yin la/_/de yi ngos gnyis rkub tshos yin/_/de yi 'og ni 'breg

[\[180a.1\]]rta ni bcu po 'di/_/gzhal min yin par shes par bya/_/stobs ma nyams shing mdzes pa yi/_/lus ldan rta ni bco lnga pa/_/lus kyi chu zhing legs gnas shing /_/gnas kyi cha ni ma 'phyugs pa/_/tshod shes pa yi gzhal bya yin/_/

[\[180b.1\]]'dra/_/so la sor gcig bzhi cha gcig_/mche ba sor gcig bzhi cha gsum/_/rna ba'i dpangs su sor lnga ru/_/rta ni bar ma'i yin par bshad/_/ske mgrin nyi shu rtsa bdun du/_/bstan bcos rtogs par shes par bya/_rngog mtha' sor ni lnga ru bshad/_/rgyab ni ske mgrin

[\[183b.3\]]de la rtswa skam dag las srang /_/brgyad brgya ta la ran pa yin/_/rlon pa de'i 'dab snye mar bcas/_/bzhi brgya nyin mtshan gcig la ran/_/thug sogs la ni bre bzhi tsam/_/bza' skam bre ni phye dang gnyis/_/snum las phul gang btung ba ni/_/bre gang sbyin par shes 
[\[183b.4\]]par bya/_/sbrang rtsi las ni phul gang sbyin/_/bur ma las ni srang brgyad sbyin/_/hwags dang kha ra gang yin pa/_/srang ni bcu yi tshad du sbyin/_/sna sman khu ba phul gang ste/_/snum gyi sna sman de yi phyed/_/du bas bdug la zho drug ste/_/du ba drag po zho gnyis tshad/_/

[\[193a.2\]]rje ngar dang ni de mtshams su/_/byung na 'dir ni smad par bshad/_/ske rtsar byung na nor thob cing /_/rngog ltag gis ni rgyal srid skyed/_/rgyu mtshams thig le nor rnyed la/_/snying gar byung na gcong can 'gyur/_/rtsib logs g.yas byung bud med rnyed/_/mjug

[\[193a.4\]]ma'i rtsa bar byung /_/nor rnams thams cad brlag par 'gyur/_/pus mor byung na 'chi ba skyed/_/lkog ma tshe'u phus lam 'gro ston/_/rmang kha'i thig les bu skye ston/_/spyi la bud med nyes pa skyed/_/tshe'u tsher byung na gnod pa skyed/_/gang zhig brtan pa'i gnas su 
[\[193a.5\]]byung /_/'dod pa thams cad skyed byed do/_/yan lag la byung don kun 'grub/_/mdza' bo 'phel zhing dpal skyed la/_/rigs dang grags pa 'phel par byed/_/mdog dang rjes mthun mdog byung shis/_/mdog gzhan shis pa ma yin no/_/ser po byung na 
[\[193a.6\]]g.yul du 'jig/_/dkar po la yang de dang 'dra/_/nag po 'thab mo skyed par byed/_/nag po byung na g.yul du 'chi/_/sdug dang mi sdug bdag gis/_/bdag po la ni 'jigs chen 'byung /_/thig le'i steng du thig le byung /_/de yang rjes mthun mdog tu min/_/
[\[193a.7\]]des ni rgyal po brlag pa dang /_/yul 'khor dag kyang 'khrug par ston/_/gang tshe rta ni dkar po la/_/thig le dkar po rnams gyur na/_/de yi bdag po rtag tu bde/_/bu dang tsha bo rnams par 'phel/_/gang zhig rta ni dmar po la/_/thig le dmar po snang gyur na/_/
[\[193b\]]
[\[193b.1\]]bdag pos mi bzad dgra 'dul zhing /_/longs sbyod chen po'ang yun ring 'gyur/_/nad kyis thebs kyang myur du 'tsho/_/gang tshe rta ni ser po la/_/thig le ser po snang gyur na/_/bdag po rigs btsun nor mang 'phel/_/gang tshe rta ni nag po la/_/thig le nag po 
[\[193b.2\]]snang gyur na/_/de yi bdag po mi bzad pa'i/_/nad kyis btab cing myur du 'chi/_/gang tshe rta ni dkar po la/_/thig le dmar po snang gyur na/_/bdag po myur du dgra yis gsod/_/thig le nag po byung ba na/_/bdag po myur du 'chi'am 'chi/_/rta dmar po la thig 
[\[193b.3\]]le ser/_/mi snang dgra bo bdag po gsod/_/rta dmar thig le nag po snang /_/de yi bdag po grags min pa'i/_/las kyis gsod par 'gyur ba yin/_/rta nag thig le dkar po snang /_/bdag po grags thob g.yul las rgyal/_/nag po dmar po'i thig le 
[\[193b.4\]]snang /_/bdag po g.yul du snyan grags thob/_/dkar la shin tu dkar ba dang /_/snum zhing mnyam pa'i thig le snang /_/de yis bdag po rgyal srid thob/_/de ni rgyal por 'gyur ba yin/_/bu dang tsha bo rnam par 'phel/_/rgyal po dang ni yul 'khor shis/_/

[\[193b.6\]]'bras ba dra ba ni/_/bdag po bu dang phrad par 'gyur/_/kha dog sna tshogs dmar shas che/_/nor rnyed par ni ston pa yin/_/mdog mi sdug cing 'thor ba dang /_/rtsub cing cha ni me mnyam dang /_/dbyibs ngan pa dang phra ba dang /_/de bzhin rgyab tu blta ba dang /_/mi mthun 
[\[193b.7\]]gnas dang mtshon chogs dang /_/mdog las mdog gzhan gyur pa rnams/_/des ni yul 'khor 'jog byed yin/_/rgyal srid nyams par byed pa yin/_/rgyal po yi ni dpung rnams dang /_/mdzod dang bu ni 'joms pa yin/_/thig le shis pa'i gnas byung yang /_/
[\[194a\]]
[\[194a.1\]]gang zhig mtshams su skyes pa dang /_/snum mdangs chung par gyur pa rnams/_/smad pa yin zhes rnam par bstan/_/snum zhing mdzes par gyur pa ni/_/shis pa thob pa kho nar bstan/_/de ltar gnas dang gnas min par/_/skyes pa'i mtshan ma 
[\[194a.2\]]kun bstan nas/_/'dir ni kha dog kun 'bras bu/_/'di ni rtse gcig yid kyis zung /_/ji ltar gnas ni rnam pa bcur/_/mtshan nyid so sor rnam par dbye/_/de bzhin thig le 'bras bu yang /_/ltas su gyur bar shes bar bya/_/de bas gnas dang ltas rnams ni/_/mtshan 
[\[194a.3\]]ma dag kyang gzung bar bya/_/de ltar thig le ma lus pa'i/_/mtshan nyid ji bzhin smras pa yin/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las thig le bzang ngan brtag pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o//_//sha li ho tra bder gnas la/_/legs thos kyis ni yang dris pa/_/
[\[194a.4\]]shar zhes bya'i khyad par glog_/dka' thub chen pos de gsungs shig_/dkar la mdog gzhan dang 'dres pa/_/sha ra zhes ni bya bar bshad/_/gang zhig kho na gzhan gyis ni/_/kha dog dkar ba zil mnan na/_/gnas dang grags pa gzhan dag gis/_/de yi 
[\[194a.5\]]bdag po zil mnan 'gyur/_/'di yang gnas dang dbyibs dag ni/_/gzhan las 'bras bu gzhan du 'gyur/_/ra ba mda' yab 'dra ba dang /_/khang ba'i phyi sgo lta bu yi/_/rta la sha ra yod na ni/_/bdag pos la dbang byed 'gyur/_/gdengs ka sbrul 'khyil 
[\[194a.6\]]shing 'dra dang /_/khyu mchog rus sbal chu srin 'dra/_/sha ra rta la yod na ni/_/bdag po shin tu phyug par 'gyur/_/kha bzang ri yi rtse mo 'dra/_/ri mo gsal po yod pa'i rta/_/bdag po dbang phyug ldan pa dang /_/rang dang gzhan nyid bkur par 'gyur/_/chag 
[\[194a.7\]]pa'i shing dang sga 'dra yod/_/bdag pos phan zad par 'gyur/_/rnga rtse dag tu byung na ni/_/bdag po'i mching ba 'phel bar 'gyur/_/gang zhig lte ba nas bzung nas/_/ro stod nyi ma shar kha 'dra/_/de bzhin ro smad cha dang ni/_/zla ba yin mdog 'dra ba/_/mdangs dang 
[\[194b\]]
[\[194b.1\]]mdog ni phun tshogs pa/_/nyin mo g.yul las rgyal byed yin/_/de las go bzlog gyur pa dang /_/mtshan mo g.yul las rgyal byed yin/_/gang gi lte ba nas bzung nas/_/ro stod mdangs snum nag po ni/_/de yang nyin mo rgyal byed

[\[194b.3\]]stod kyi ni mdog 'dra zhing /_/mdangs snum shis pa'i gzugs can ni/_/rta de bsngags par gyur pa yin/_/bram ze'am ni rgyal rigs las/_/gzhan gyis de thob mi rigs so/_/des ni dgra kun zad byed yin/_/chos dang 'dod pa'ang ster byed yin/_/gang 
[\[194b.4\]]gi ro stod mdog nag cing /_/ro smad mdangs snum dkar ba ni/_/dgra 'joms mdza' bo 'phel byed de/_/de yang g.yul las rgyal byed yin/_/ro stod dung ltar dkar ba la/_/ro smad mdog zhan ldan pa yin/_/de la mdza' skyes zhes bya ste/_/rigs 'phel nyin 
[\[194b.5\]]mo shis pa yin/_/par la lta ra mchu dmar zhing /_/kha dang lce'u chung dmar gyur pa/_/khyim du'ang shis par 'gyur ba dang /_/lam du'ang phan pa skyed byed yin/_/gzhan yang de dang 'dra ba'i rta/_/mdog la spu ni srab ba dang /_/mdangs snum shis pa'i gzugs can 
[\[194b.6\]]ni/_/rta mchog de ni bsngags pa yin/_/gnag dang dmar dang ser po la/_/zlum ris dkar pos brgyan pa'am/_/dkar la mdog gzhan gyis brgyan pa'i/_/rta ni dpal skyed byed pa yin/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las sha ra'i mdog gi khyad par zhes bya ba'i le'u

[\[195a\]]
[\[195a.1\]]shis pa'i/_/mtshan nyid thams cad shes bya ba/_/so sor rgyas pa dag tu ni/_/legs ldan gyis ni sngar bshad na/_/'di dag thams cad ma lus pa/_/bsres pa dag kyang rtogs bar 'tshal/_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/mtshan

[\[195a.7\]]bar skye/_/byi ba'i sder mo zhes bshad do/_/rta de mja' dang 'brel byed yin/_/gzhung thig can dang mig mi sdug_/de bzhin byi ba'i sder mo can/_/mdog mi sdug pa'i rta mthong na/_/ring du yongs su sbang bar bya/_/rdza gyo chag pa'i mdog 'dra ba'i/_/thal ba'i 
[\[195b\]]
[\[195b.1\]]mdog dang byi la'i mdog_/byi ba'i mdog dang mdog mi sdug_/de dag gis ni bdag po gsod/_/don grub pa la smad pa yin/_/du ba du ba mig mdog 'dres/_/me tog kha se tA la'i mdog 'dra ba/_/rnga ma rngog ma dmar ser yod/_/rta de rigs ni rlag byed yin/_/
[\[195b.2\]]gang gi rlig pa rmig pa dang /_/rnga ma mig dang rkan yang gnag_/de ni mjug gnag ces bya ste/_/rigs dang mdzod ni zad byed yin/_/phrag so rgyab dang ske rtsa dang /_/ong do dag tu [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]ni/_/e na ya yi mdog ltar gnag_/de ni g.yang gzhi can zhes bya/_/phrag 
[\[195b.3\]]sos bu ni 'chi bar 'gyur/_/ske rtsas kha lo ba 'chi 'gyur/_/rgyab kyis bdag po 'chi bar 'gyur/_/ong dos chung ma ngan spyod 'gyur/_/gang zhig stag 'dra'i ri mo yong /_/rta de blun po zhes bya ste/_/gser can zhes kyang de la bya/_/g.yul du bdag po gsod byed yin/

[\[195b.5\]]gcig dkar mdangs med pa/_/bdag po gsod byed g.yul du spang /_/rkang lag gnyis ni dkar gyur pa/_/g.yul du shis shing dpal skyed min/_/rkang lag dkar ba'i 'bras bu ni/_/rgya cher nga yis bshad par bya/_/rta gang gis ni mdun rkang gi_/g.yas pa dkar po 
[\[195b.6\]]gyur pa de/_/g.yul du don mi 'grub par byed/_/snga rkang g.yon pa dkar gyur pa/_/dpung ba gnod pa skyed byed yin/_/phyi rkang gis ni g.yas pa dkar/_/bdag po gsod par byed pa yin/_/g.yon pa dkar bsag yul du ni/_/zhon pa po dang bcas btson 'khyer/_/
[\[195b.7\]]snga rkang gnyis dang de bzhin du/_/phyi rkang gi ni g.yon pa dkar/_/bdag po g.yul du mda' mdung gis/_/'chi bar byed pa nyid yin no/_/snga rkang gnyis dang de bzhin du/_/phyi rkang g.yas pa dkar gyur pa/_/g.yul du bdag po 'chi gyur nas/_/de yi rjes la rang nyid 'chi/

[\[196b\]]
[\[196b.1\]]lha yi rta ni de 'dra 'byung /_/de yi rgyal po rgyal byed cing /_/dgra rnams thul bar byed pa dang /_/g.yul du rtag tu shis pa dang /_/nor dang bu dang tsha bor ldan/_/gang zhig skyes pa tshun tshad ni/_/rnga rngog skye bar mi 'gyur ba/_/bdag po rigs ni brlag byas nas/_/de yi

[\[196b.3\]]bu'am/_/lag sor lta bu'i rwa dag ni/_/sbom po'am yang na phra mo dag_/rid dang thung dang rtsub dang ljang /_/sra dang snyi ba gang yang rung /_/rna rtsa'am skra mtshams tha ma'am/_/dpral pa yang na spyi bo'am/_/de las gzhan pa'i gnas dag tu/_/de las gzhan pa'i dbyibs yod 
[\[196b.4\]]pa/_/gang byung de ni rwa can de/_/bdag po yul 'khor 'jig byed yin/_/yul phyogs gang na rta de yod/_/yul de rlag par 'gyur ba yin/_/des na de ni dgra bo yi/_/yul du bskyal la rjes zhi bya/_/rta ni gang gi kha la ni/_/sha khra yis ni kun du khyab/_/de bzhin rkub dang rlig 
[\[196b.5\]]pa dang /_/rna ba dag dang mig tu byung /_/rta de sha khra can zhes bya/_/mi shis sdig pa'i mtshan nyid can/_/rta bres su ni mig gzhug cing /_/bkra shis 'dod pas reg mi bya/_/mig gi nyes pa bshad bya yi/_/khyi mig dom dang byi ba'i mig_/sbal mig nor bu'i mig 'dra dang /_/byi ba 
[\[196b.6\]]'ug pa nya mig can/_/phag dang khyu mchog lta bu'i mig_/bya rog lug dang khra mig can/_/khra yi mig can smad pa yin/_/mig yo mig ni gdug pa dang /_/mig ser ba yang spang bar bya/_/des ni bdag po mi bde zhing /_/rigs zad pa yang byed pa yin/_/g.yul du bdag

[\[197a\]]
[\[197a.1\]]na mi shis gyur pa yin/_/rkan ni snum zhing zab pa dang /_/dmar par gyur na bsngags pa yin/_/shin tu mdar zhing cher khyab la/_/rtsub mo med la'ang 'bras bu che/_/snum chung dmar pa'i sha chung ba/_/'bras bu chung bar shes par bya/_/rkan

[\[197a.3\]]'bras shin tu 'phyang ba dang /_/g.yul du bdag po gsod par byed/_/rlig pa spu ni che ba'am/_/yang na ser skyar gyur pa'am/_/de bzhin zhu par gyur pa yang /_/da yis bdag po gsod byed yin/_/kha dog gzhan dang ldan pa las/_/pus mo gnyis ni nag gyur cing /_/

[\[197a.6\]]'bar bzhes bya dang /_/rnga rtse nag pa ma gtogs pa/_/rta ni kha dog nag pa la/_/dkar po cung zad yod pa shis/_/lham bu chu shel mdog 'dra zhing /_/gang gi mgo ni dkar gyur pa/_/rta de dpal yon can zhes bya/_/rgyal po rnam rgyal skyed byed yin/_sha li ho 
[\[197a.7\]]tra'i mdo'i gnas las 'dres pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'o//_//kha ba can gyi ri bo'i mchog_/'byung khungs sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa/_/shin tu mtho zhing rtse mo che/_/kun nas rlung ni mi ldang zhing /_/rtse mo kha bas rnam par g.yogs/_/mi'am ci yi glu 
[\[197b\]]
[\[197b.1\]]sgras khyab/_/bya rnams sna tshogs sgra 'byin cing /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sna tshogs rnams kyis 'dzes/_/sna tshogs shing gis kun du khyab/_/mkha' la rgyu ba rtag tu dga'/_/rig 'dzin chogs ni legs gnas shing /_/dri za'i tshogs kyis bsten par ni/_/sha li ho tra legs 
[\[197b.2\]]gnas la/_/legs thos kyis ni yang dris pas/_/rgyal po'i bzhon par 'os pa'i rta/_/drang srong chen pos bdag la gsungs/_/rgyal po'i rta ni ci lta bu/_/de yi nges pa bdag la gsungs/_/bu yis de skad dris pa dang /_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/yon tan skyon 
[\[197b.3\]]dag rgyal po la/_/'jig rten 'byung ba ci 'dra bar/_/de ltar tha ma mi rnams la/_/'byung mi 'byung bar gzhung las bshad/_/de bas rgyal po'i bzhon pa yi/_/mtshan nyid 'bad pas brtag par bya/_/ci nas nyes pa mi 'byung bar/_/rnam pa kun du bya ba yin/_/tshad dang stobs dang 
[\[197b.4\]]snying stobs gzi/_/stobs dang lus kyi me drod ldan/_/gtsug 'khyil bzang zhing mdangs rab snang /_/'gros na phun sum tshogs pa dang /_/skal bzang dri ni zhim pa dang /_/de bzhin skye gnas bzang ba dang /_/rig ldan dang tshul phun sum tshogs/_/dul zhing mthong na dga' ba dang /_/
[\[197b.5\]]bad kan rang bzhin can mchog dang /_/de bzhin bzang po'i skye gnas dang /_/smad ba'i mtshan nyid med gyur cing /_/kun nas rnam pa thams cad du/_/mtshan nyid bzang po phun tshogs pa/_/rab tu 'bad de brtag par bya/_/rgyal po'i bzhon par 'os pa'i rta/_/rgyal po'i 
[\[197b.6\]]rta zhes bya bar bshad/_/gang zhig gzhan kun med pa yi/_/brtan pa'i gtsug ni bcud dang ldan/_/rgyal po'i bzhon par 'os pa'i rta/_/ma li ka dang 'dra ba'i mig_/mdog snum rab tu dkar ba'i rta/_/zla ba'i lha dang ldan pa yin/_/rgyal po de la zhon na ni/_/sa steng 
[\[197b.7\]]dbang du byed par 'gyur/_/ne tsho mdog can nyi ma'i rta/_/rgyal po'i g.yul ngor zhon na ni/_/de yi don rnams 'grub 'gyur zhing /_/sa yang dbang du byed par 'gyur/_/de bzhin rgyal po'i mdog dmar rta/_/rnam thos sras kyi lha can te/_/de la bzhon nas rnams ni/_/ma 
[\[198a\]]
[\[198a.1\]]lus dbang byed the tshom med/_/sngo bsangs lha dbang rta yin te/_/mdangs snum ldan la rgyal po ni/_/zhon na sa ni ma lus las/_/rtag tu rgyal bar nges pa yin/_/gnag cing 'tsher ba'i mdangs dang ldan/_/rta mchog de ni chu lha'i yin/_/de la zhon na rgyal 
[\[198a.2\]]po ni/_/myur du nor dang 'bru 'phel 'gyur/_/mdog dkar rna ba sngo ba dang /_/rngog ma dang ni rnga ma gnag_/chu lha'i rta ni gnyis pa ste/_/de rnyed pa ni ma lus 'thob/_/lham bu chu shel ltar snang ba/_/rta ni nor lha'i yin par bshad/_/rgyal po de la zhon na ni/_/myur du nor mang 
[\[198a.3\]]bdag por 'gyur/_/ba so'i mdog 'dra rdam gnag_/kun nas mdangs ni snum dang ldan/_/chos kyi lda nges se zhon na/_/rgyal po mchod sbyin byed par 'gyur/_/lung tang snum po dang 'dra ba/_/rta mchog de ni gshin rje'i yin/_/rgyal po de la zhon gyur na/_/myur du dgra kun 
[\[198a.4\]]las rgyal 'gyur/_/rma bya'i mdangs dang 'dra ba yi/_/ri mos khyab par gyur pa'i rta/_/sna tshogs lha'i yin te zhon na/_/'dod pa thams cad thob par 'gyur/_/de bzhin ma rgad bcag pa yi/_/mdog ltar shin tu dmar pa'i rta/_/khyab 'jug rta ste de zhon na/_/myur du sa ni 
[\[198a.5\]]dbang byed 'gyur/_/de bzhin gser dang 'dra ba la/_/'tsher bas rnam par brgyan gyur pa/_/dbang phyug chen po'i rta zhon na/_/dgra rnams dbang du 'gyur pa yin/_/de bzhin shel dang 'dra ba ru/_/dang zhing mdangs bcas lus yod pa/_/rnyed na mchod sbyin dam pa yi/_/skal 
[\[198a.6\]]brnyed par 'gyur pa yin/_/gang zhig nag la mgo bo dang /_/rkang pa bzhin ni dkar gyur pa/_/shes ldan rta de rnyed na ni/_/nad kyis zil gnon mi 'gyur ro/_/lham bu chu shel 'dra ba la/_/sngar zhing yan lag lnga dkar ba/_/rgyal po de la zhon na ni/_/phun sum tshogs pa thams 
[\[198a.7\]]cad thob/_/lus kyis mdog ni gzhan 'dra zhing /_/mtshan nyid thams cad ldan pa li/_/tha skar bu yi rta rnyed na/_/rta ni mang bo rnyed par 'gyur/_/dkar dang gnag pa'i tshul gyis khra/_/g.yas logs dkar ba'i shas che la/_/g.yon pa khru bor gyur pa li/_/rta ni khra bor rnyed 
[\[198b\]]
[\[198b.1\]]gyur na/_/rta de'i lha ni ngan sbong ste/_/'dod pa thams cad rab sgrub yin/_/lham bu chu shel 'dra'i mdog la/_/ne tsho'i mdog 'dra'i 'khor ling can/_/drag po'i rta de rnyed na ni/_/'dod pa thams cad thob par 'gyur/_/de lta bu yi rgyal ba'i rta/_/rgyal po yi ni rtag tu bya/_/
[\[198b.2\]]de yi gnas ni rta gzhan dag_/gang dang yang ni gcig mi bya/_/ngo mtshar mdog dang ldan pa'i rta/_/dmod pas sa steng skyes pa yin/_/dri za'i bur ni shes gyis la/_/rab tu 'bad de bsnyen bkur bya/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las rgyal po'i bzhon pa'i rta'i mtshan 
[\[198b.3\]]nyid brtags pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gsum pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/rta yi mig gi mtshan nyid do/_/gang zhig baiDUr+ya 'dra'i mdog_/de bzhin shel gyi mdog 'dra dang /_/phug ron gyi ni mig 'dra dang /_/ka la ping+ga'i mig 'dra dang /_/glang chen 
[\[198b.4\]]rma bya rnga mo dang /_/sreg pa'i mig dang 'dra ba dang /_/gser gyi mdog dang 'dra ba nyid/_/de yi mig ni shis pa yin/_/skyon med pa yi chig la ni/_/rdzi ma phra mos khyab pa dang /_/mig 'bras legs shing ma snang la/_/thur du bltas par ma gyur pa/_/mdog ni bzang po 
[\[198b.5\]]dang ldan zhing /_/mig gi mdangs dang ldan pa'i rta/_/de ni g.yul las rgyal byed pa'i/_/rgyal ba yi ni bzhon par 'gyur/_/yangs shing snum la mnyam pa dang /_/me tog ma li ka 'dra dang /_/tham ring mo yong gyur cing /_/dbus na yongs su mdzes pa dag_/rta de ma 
[\[198b.6\]]li ka'i mig can/_/mig zur cung zad gnag pa yin/_/de yis mdza' bo 'phel 'gyur zhing /_/rta dang mdzod kyang rgyas par 'gyur/_/dkar ba'am mdog rjes dkar gyur la/_/mig la skyon med mthong mig nag_/rdzi ma mig dkyus nag gyur ba/_/gang la yod pa 
[\[198b.7\]]der nor skyed/_/mig ni shel du 'dra ba la/_/rgyal mo'i rtsa ba gnag gyur cing /_/mig gi rgyal mo rdul med pas/_/de ni shel mig can zhes bya/_/mig dang lus kyi mdog 'dra ba/_/mig gi mdog can zhes bya dge_/kha dog lus dang tha dad kyang /_/gang gi mig 
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]ni bzang gyur cing /_/mi sdug pa dang ma tshang med/_/de ni mig bzang nor 'phel byed/_/kha dog lus dang tha dad cing /_/smad par bya pa'i mig yod pa/_/mig ngan pa yi rta gang yin/_/las rnams kun la smad pa yin/_/de bas dbyibs 
[\[199a.2\]]dang tshad ldan zhing /_/dpe dang legs bar 'dra ba dang /_/rdzi ma'i kha dog snum pa yi/_/mig gang yin pa de bsngags so/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las mig brtag pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa bzhi ba'o//_//bsgrub dang grub ba'i rig 'dzin gtso/_/drang srong 
[\[199a.3\]]gzi brjid chen po la/_/yon tan nges kyis yongs dris pa/_/rta so'i rig byed mkhyen lags sam/_/de skad dris tshe drang srong mchog_/sha li ho tra'i slang smras ba/_/rta so'i rig byed shes lags so/_/bu khyod kyis ni nyan par gyis/_/mdun so thams cad thags 
[\[199a.4\]]bzang snyoms/_/sra zhing mtshan ma rab tu gsal/_/snum pa'am sbrang rtsi'i mdog 'dra ba'i/_/rta yi so ni bsngags pa yin/_/mche ba so dang mdog 'dra dang /_/'gram so rnams kyang de dang 'dra/_/so rnams gting rid srab pa dang /_/ri mo phra zhing shin tu 'jam/_/gos 
[\[199a.5\]]pa med cing rtsa ba yi/_/mdog dang kun tu 'dra ba shis/_/mnyam zhing yan lag rab tu 'byor/_/chu zheng cha de dag ldan pa dang /_/gru gzugs ni shin tu legs gyur pa'i/_/so ni bde ba skyed byed yin/_/so ni lhag pa yod pa'i rta/_/bdag po yul mi rnams la gnod/_/de 
[\[199a.6\]]bzhin so ni ma tshang ba/_/bdag po chung mar bcas brlag_/so yis kha ni khengs gyur ba/_/rigs rlag byed pas spang bar byed/_/so rtsa so dang bcas pa ni/_/mi shis pa yi gzugs ldan zhing /_/log las byung bar gyur ba dag_/gtsigs pa de ni rigs rlag byed/_/
[\[199a.7\]]rta yi so ni gnag gyur ba/_/bdag po'i nor ni rlag byed yin/_/mtho dman khyer dang sbom phra khyer/_/de bzhin phred ni rnam par sbangs/_/mi mnyam med ces bya so de/_/bdag po'i bu ni gsod byed yin/_/rtse mo nang du grub pa yi/_/so ni nor rnams 'jig byed yin/_/
[\[199b\]]
[\[199b.1\]]thams cad dbyung ba'i chag pa'am/_/gang zhig so ni sngon du 'dus/_/de ni shin tu nyes chen skyed/_/mdog dang dbyibs ni nya phyis 'dra/_/so de nya phyis can zhes bya/_/nya phyis so can rta yis ni/_/zas dang sgom la gnod pa skyed/_/'og so shin tu gyis gyur na/_/bdag 
[\[199b.2\]]po ltogs ba'i 'jigs pa 'byung /_/ya so shin tu gyis gyur na/_/bdag po dgra las pham par 'gyur/_/zur gsum rtse mo rno ba'i so/_/sga dang nor ni 'phrog byed yin/_/sa dang bcas par skyes gyur na/_/de ni thams cad zad byed yin/_/de dag las no go bzlog 
[\[199b.3\]]pa'i/_/so bzang 'bras bu shis pa yin/_/mche ba dang ni 'gram so yi/_/'bras bu de bzhin shes par gyis/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las so brtag pa'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa lnga pa'o//_//me dang nyi ma'i mdog 'dra ba/_/dka' thub can de sdig pa 
[\[199b.4\]]med/_/thub mchog sha li ho tral/_/mja' las rgyal gyis yang dris pa/_/brtan pa'i gtsug ni dur shes bya/_/glo bur bas ni du zhig yin/_/shes dang shes bya'i bdag po yis/_/gtsug rnams kyi ni mtshan nyid ni/_/gang du ji ltar gnas pa yin/_/gtsug rnams 
[\[199b.5\]]kyi ni dbyibs gyur pa/_/rnam pa dur ni 'gyur ba gsungs/_/gtsug gi tshad ni ji tsam zhig_/shes dang smad pa'i grangs ni du/_/thams cad gnas las 'phro ba dang /_/g.yas pa dang ni g.yon pa dang /_/stabs pa dang ni sman pa yi/_/'bras bu 'od zer ci ltar 
[\[199b.6\]]'gyur/_/gzhan yang lus kyi mtshan nyid ni/_/brtan dang mi brtan ji lta bu/_/lus las skyes pa'i gnas rnams ni/_/gnas kyi gang zhig gnas skabs dang /_/rkan gyi kha dog bye brag gi_/glo bur byung ba'i 'bras bu dang /_/srab dang mthug pa'i spu rnams ni/_/
[\[199b.7\]]gtsug la ji ltar 'byung ba lags/_/de skang thub mchog 'dris tshe/_/sha li ho tras khu la smras/_/'di na brtan dang mi brtan dang /_/yan lag rnam pa brgyad yin la/_/'bras bu 'byung ba'i tshul gyis ni/_/mtshan nyid rab tu shes par bya/_/shis dang rma bya'i mtshan 
[\[200a\]]
[\[200a.1\]]nyid ni/_/thams cad lus la brten pa yin/_/lus las 'byung bar gyur pa ni/_/lus dang yan lag rnam pa gnyis/_/dge ba'i gnas su shis pa'i gtsug_/rgya che ba yi 'bras bu ster/_/mi shis gnas ni ngan pa 'byung /_/shin tu mi shis skal 
[\[200a.2\]]chen 'gyur/_/tshad dang ldan zhing gsal bar byung /_/mtshan nyid ldan pa'i 'bas bu che/_/tshad mi ldan zhing mi gsal ba/_/de'i 'dras bu chung bar 'dod/_/gnas dang dbyibs kyi bye breg las/_/mtshan nyid 'bras bu chen por 'gyur/_/sor gnyis las lhag nya phyis 
[\[200a.3\]]te/_/sor gnyis pa ni lhag ces bya/_/shing lham 'dra dang rkang brgya pa/_/sor ni phyed dang gnyis pa yin/_/shing lham phyed ni nas drug zhing /_/'dres pa sor ni brgyad pa yin/_/de yi cha gsum phri ba ni/_/'khyil ba zhes ni bya bar bshad/_bzhi cha pri par gyur pa ni/
[\[200a.4\]]kha 'bus pa zhes bya ba yin/_/de las lhag pa'am ma tshad ba'i/_/gtsug ni 'bras bu med pa yin/_/pags srab spu ni phra ba dang /_/lus kyi gsang ba'i gnas su byung /_/de ni ngo bo nyid kyis phra/_/tshad dang ldan rung mi ldan rung /_/'bras bu bcas par shes par bya/_/
[\[200a.5\]]phra ba de dag gnod byed yin/_/yan lag brgyad pa'i mtshan nyid kyis/_/shis dang mi shis 'og nas bshad/_/rta yi gnas bcu'i spu tshogs la/_/bstan pa gang yin der shes bya/_/mchu sgros rdog mar re re dang /_/gtsug dang brang la gnyis gnyis te/_/rgyu zhabs 
[\[200a.6\]]rkub lhag gnyis gnyis te/_/bcu po de dag ldan par bshad/_/kun nas rdzogs shing shin tu gsal/_/gtsug ni g.yas su 'khyil ba dang /_/chung ba ma yin che ba min/_/gser ltar mdangs snum 'od dang bcas/_/dbyibs ni legs par skyes pa dag_/las rnams 
[\[200a.7\]]thams cad sgrub byed yin/_/legs thos the tshom skyes gyur nas/_/drang srol de la slar smras pa/_/ji ltar bcu po nyid ldan gyi/_/gzhan dag de ltar ma yin bshad/_/rta rnams skyes zin gyur nas ni/_/gtsug de nam yang mi skye la/_/skyes pa 'jig par mi 'gyur zhing 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]gnas las 'pho bar mi 'gyur ro/_/skyes tshe lhan cig skye bar 'gyur/_/de bas thams cad bstan pa yin/_/legs thos tshig ni thos ba dang /_/de rjes drang srong gis smras pa/_/ji ltar phyogs der skyes gyur pa/_/de ltar gzhan du ma yin pa/_/pha mang yod dang med gyur kyang /_/cig 
[\[200b.2\]]shos la ni dbang ba yin/_/phal cher gnas bcu dbang gyur gyi/_/de ltar gzhan du mi snang ngo /_/de bas bcu po bstan par bya/_/des pa nyid du zung bar gyis/_/'di dag la med nges 'byung ba/_/gzhan dag de ltar min par bshad/_/brtan dang ma brtan gang yin kyang /_/'bras 
[\[200b.3\]]bu rnam pa gnyis su bshad/_/gnas ni dgu bcu rtsa drug yin/_/dbyibs ni rgyal po nyid yin no/_/'dres dag kha 'bus 'khyil ba dang /_/rkang pa brgya dang lhag pa dang /_/shing lha ma dang 'dra shing lha ma phyed/_/nya phyis 'dra ste rnam pa brgyad/_/smad pa'am de sngags pa'i gnas 
[\[200b.4\]]dag gam/_/gnas su bstan dang ma bstan gang /_/gtsug 'khyil pa yi dbyibs byung na/_/de ni rjes mthun 'bras bu skyed/_/'khyil ba chu skor lta bu yin/_/nya phyis nya phyis dbyibs ltar 'ong /_/dbyibs gcig gnyis sam mang por ni/_/du ma gang tshe 'dus gyur pa'i/_/
[\[200b.5\]]gtsug 'khyil de ni 'dres pa'am/_/'dus pa zhes ni bya bar bshad/_/spu ni 'gro tshe me tog dag_/kha 'bus ltar snang lhag pa yin/_/zlum po'am yang na zur bzhi ni/_/shing lham dbyibs 'dra shing lham can/_/brad ba'am yang na rting 
[\[200b.6\]]pa'i gnas/_/byed 'dra shing lham phyed pa yin/_/me tog mgo lcogs dbyibs 'dra ba/_/de ni rkang brgyad bar shes bya/_/dbyibs las mi mthun gyur pa ni/_/gzhan dag kyang ni snang mod kyi/_/de ni spu yi 'gyur ba yin/_/gnas las shis dang mi shis 'gyur/_/gtsug kyang 
[\[200b.7\]]gnas dang dbyibs dang ni/_/mi shis mtshan nyid shes par bya/_/cha dang 'phos dang 'thor ba dang /_/gtsug ni 'phred du 'byung ba dang /_/lhag pa'am chad par gyur pa'am/_/gang yang gnas las 'pho 'gyur pa'am/_/dbyibs ngan mdog ni mi sdug pa/_/brtan po rnams ni mi snang 
[\[201a\]]
[\[201a.1\]]pa'am/_/smad pa'i gnas su g.yas 'khyil dang /_/bsngags pa'i gnas su g.yon 'khyil ba/_/mtshan nyid ngan pa yin zhes bya/_/des ni tshe thung skyed par byed/_/gtsug gi gnas ni dgu bcu drug_/yod pa bshad pa de rnams ni/_/shis dang smad bar 
[\[201a.2\]]bya ba ste/_/rnam pa gnyis su bsdu bar bya/_/de las gzhan ni nyi shu zhig_/shis pa yin par shes par bya/_/bdun cu rtsa drug smad bya ste/_/de ltar dgu bcu rtsa drug yin/_/kha 'gram la gnyis dbral bar bzhi/_/dpral mtshams la gcig kho na yin/_/rna rtsar 
[\[201a.3\]]gnyis te lkog mar gcig_/mgo po'i mthar ni gnyis yin la/_/brang du gsum ste dpung par gnyis/_/mid pa gcig ste de bzhin du/_/rgyu dang long bu gnyis gnyis su/_/gtsug ni 'byung bar shes par bya/_/de ltar shis par gyur pa'i gnas/_/nyi shu yin 
[\[201a.4\]]par rnam par bshad/_/gtsug ni 'di rnams gnyis la ni/_/sum cu rtsa bdun grangs su 'gyur/_/mur 'gram mur gong mchi stan dang /_/mkhur tshos sna phor smin ma dang /_/rna rtsa lce'u ldang rtsi'u chung dang /_/mchan khur dku dang 'gram sre long /_sna rtse pus mo 
[\[201a.5\]]mig 'bras gnyis/_/rtsib logs rkub tshos dag tu ni/_/gnas ni gnyis gnyis dag tu gtsug_/gnyis gnyis 'byung ba sum cu drug_/sna gdong mchu ltag srol mdud dang /_/dpung sna snying ga ze'u drug mdo/_/lte ba rkub dang mjug srub dang /_/ya mchu ze'u ltag sga 
[\[201a.6\]]'og ste/_/de ltar gnas ni de dag tu/_/gtsug ni bcu gsum 'byung ba yin/_/tshe'u spu de bzhin tshigs grangs dang /_/tshe'u tshe gcig phra mo yi/_/gnas ni bcu drug 'di dag tu/_/gtsug kyang bcu drug nyid yin no/_/an dang ru dang ske ba'i rtsa/_/rmang kha rnga mar gnas 
[\[201a.7\]]gnyis gnyis/_/de ltar gnas brgyad ldan pa ru/_/gtsug ni rnam ba'ang brgyad yin no/_/mchu sgros de yi so gnyis te/_/gnas kyi gsum po 'di dag tu/_/gtsug ni gsum po 'byung pa yin/_/de phyir dgu bcu drug gsal nas/_/de ltar bstan zhing dge ba dang /_/smad 
[\[201b\]]
[\[201b.1\]]pa dag kyang bshad pa yin/_/gtsug rnams kyi ni khyad par gyis/_/'bras bu 'byung ba mnyan par bgyi/_/ya mtshur gnas pa'i gtsug gis ni/_/bdag po'i blon po gsod bar byed/_/mchu sgos steng dang g.yas kyis ni/_/bdag po'i pha ni 'chi bar 'gyur/_/de yi g.yon na 
[\[201b.2\]]yod pas ni/_/zhon pa po yi ma 'chi byed/_/mchu ltas gong du gtsug byung na/_/bdag po'i rigs dang bcas pa sreg_/sna phor la ni g.yas pa yis/_/'tsho ba'i sgo ni 'gag par byed/_/gang gi mkhur tshos gnyis ka la/_/gtsug 'khyil ba ni snang gyur na/_/de yi 
[\[201b.3\]]bdag po'i bu thu bo/_/rta yis za bar 'gyur ba yin/_/rta gdong 'gram dang sna gdong la/_/gang gi gtsug ni snang gyur pa/_/rta de'i bdag po g.yul du ni/_/dgra bos gsod par 'gyur ba yin/_/gang gi rta la mchi stan du/_/gtsug 'khyil ba ni gnas gyur pa/_/gang la 
[\[201b.4\]]'di 'dra'i rta yod pa/_/de'i rigs rtag tu ngu bar 'gyur/_/gang gi rta yi mu 'gram gnyis/_/gtsug ni gsal bor snang gyur pa/_/de lta'i gtsug can bcang gyur na/_/des ni rigs ni gnyis su 'gyes/_/gang gi gtsug gi smin ma yi/_/bar du gnas par snang gyur pa/_/
[\[201b.5\]]rta de khro gnyer can zhes bya/_/nye du mdza' bshes rnams la gnod/_/gang gi mur gong gnyis su ni/_/gtsug ni re re snang gyur pa/_/de yis rigs kun zad byed cing /_/bdag po'i phongs pa skyed byed yin/_/'gram glang tse'u chung dag gam ni/_/tshe'u spu'am ske rtsa chig grang phra/_/
[\[201b.6\]]mtshan gnyis dag tu gtsug 'khyil ba/_/spu ni g.yon du 'khyil gyur na/_/de yis rta dang bdag po ni/_/mda' char gyis ni 'chi bar 'gyur/_/gang gi rta yi rna ba'i rtse/_/gnyi gar gtsug ni snang gyur ba/_/gsang 'phel ba yi nyes pa yis/_/de yi bdag po 'chi bar 'gyur/_/
[\[201b.7\]]gang gi rta la rna rtsa ru/_/gnas pa'i gtsug ni snang gyur dang /_/ze'u lhad la yod pa'i rta/_/bdag po'i don sgrub ma yin no/_/gang gi rta yi srong gong dbus/_/gtsug 'khyil ba ni snang gyur ba/_/de yi bdag po bza' ba dang /_/btung ba re ba 'grub mi 'gyur/_/an 
[\[202a\]]
[\[202a.1\]]dong rur ni gtsug 'khyil ba/_/rta ni gang la snang gyur pa/_/bdag po'am bu'am 'dul sbyor mkhan/_/gang yang rung la gnod pa skyed/_/gang gi ze'ur gtsug byung ba/_/rta de phongs pa dang bcas pa dang /_/bdag po'i rigs rgyud bcas brlag pa'am/_/rigs 
[\[202a.2\]]kyi gtso la gnas gyur ba/_/rta dang nor ni rlag byed cing /_/rgyal po'i yul 'khor bcas 'chi dang /_/don kun 'jig cing 'dug par na/_/skyes pa'i tshe ni nyid na bskrad/_/lag gis reg dad mig gis mthong /_thag ring du ni sbang bar bya/_/de ni dgra la sbyin 
[\[202a.3\]]pa'am/_/gzhan gyi yul 'khor dag tu bsgyur/_/gang gi ze'u ltag gtsug yod pa/_/de yi bdag po khwa'am ni/_/sems can mi sdug pas zos shing /_/g.yul du dpung dang bcas par 'chi/_/sga 'og gtsug gis bdag po 'chi/_/gsal shing bskyon te 'chi bar 'gyur/_/
[\[202a.4\]]nyen pas nyen cing nor brlag pa'am/_/yang na mtshon gyis 'chi bar byed/_/gang gi gtsug ni snying ga'am/_/dpung sna dag tu long gyur pa/_/rta de bdag po 'khor bcas par/_/g.yul du 'chi bar byed pa yin/_/pus mo'i gtsug gis g.yul du ni/_/'khrul zhing 
[\[202a.5\]]dpung bcas dgra bos gsod/_/rgyal bo rkun ma chad pa mes/_/bdag po'i rigs ni rlag par byed/_/gang gis tshig gang gnyis la ni/_/gtsug ni gnas par snang gyur na/_/de yi bdag po gnyen bcas pa/_/lcags sgrog gis ni bcing bar 'gyur/_/gang gi tshe'u'i tshe

[\[202b.1\]]kyis yongs su gdungs par 'gyur/_/rmang khar gtsug ni yod gyur pa'i/_/bdag po chung ma mi rigs spyod/_/rlig par gang gi gtsug 'khyil ba/_/rta de'i bdag por gyur pa ni/_/bu mang tshe ni thung gyur te/_/rta de blta na mi sdug yin/_/rta gang gi ni drug mdo yi/_/

[\[211b.1\]]'di rnams rnam pa du ru grags/_/mtshan nyid gang dag yin pa dag_/'di dag thams cad shes par 'tshal/_/de skad smras tshe drang srong mchog_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/khyed kyi yid la gang gnas pa/_/de ni bshad kyis mnyan bar gyis/_/rgyug pa med bar rta rnams ni/_/rtsig

[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]na/_/rta la'ang ston ka pa zhes bshad/_/rang bzhin de 'drar gyur pa yi/_/ston ka rnam pa bzhi ru mthong /_/rang bzhin la gnas de bzhin sbom/_/phra dang dmar ba'i ston ka pa'o/_/rang bzhin la gnas mchog yin te/_/phra dang sbom dang gzhan pa yin/_/rte'u ston ka 
[\[220b.2\]]dmar po ni/_/sbang bar bya bar gyur pa yin/_/phra dang sbom pa phrad pa las/_/sbom pa'am yang na phra bar 'gyur/_/phal cher gang gi mtshan nyid gang /_/de las lhag par mi 'gyur ro/_/skye gnas snying stobs lus kyi ni/_/mtshan nyid dang ni rtags dag las/_/
[\[220b.3\]]'di rnams kyi ni las dag kyang /_/thams can rim bzhin bshad par bya/_/rgod ma'i sems stobs nye ba dang /_/lam du zhugs par gyur ba yis/_/khur dang gom pa dag gis ni/_/de ni tsha bas gdungs gyur cing /_/bkres dang skom bas nyen pas na/_/mang la 
[\[220b.4\]]nas khrag ni 'dzag par 'gyur/_/zla ba drug ni 'das pa na/_/sha ni khrag tu 'gyur ba yin/_/mngal de'i tshil ni gang yin pa'ang /_/phra mo tsam zhig yod mi 'gyur/_/rus phra sha ni srab ba ru/_/skye ba ston ka dmar po yin/_/'di ni longs spyod mi rung zhing /_/las rnams 
[\[220b.5\]]nus pa'am yin no/_/mngal ni chags par gyur ba'i rta/_/zas kyi bya bas 'phel mi 'gyur/_/rtsub cing rno ba za ba dang /_/se bzhin lam du zhugs pa dang /_/khur gyis dub par byas pa dang /_/bcings dang rta pho dang sbyar dang /_/der ni mngal chags gyur pa yis/_/khrag 
[\[220b.6\]]zad log par 'gyur pa yin/_/ji ltar char med sman rnams ni/_/skye bar 'gyur ba ma yin la/_/'bad med me tog 'bras bu sogs/_/'phel bar 'gyur pa'ang ma yin no/_/de bzhin log par gsos pa yis/_/rta yi mngal ni skams par 'gyur/_/de ni sbeg cing

[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]pa'i ston ka bar/_/shes bya de yi las nyon cig_/rte'u de ni mar ram nim/_/yang na 'o mas gso bya zhing /_/stobs skyed pa ni gzhan dag ni/_/phra ba'i ston ka gso bar bya/_/ma ni 'bras tshan yu sha dang /_/de bzhin khu ba sna tshogs dang /_/
[\[221a.2\]]de bzhin btung ba sna tshogs dang /_/bsku dang 'jam rtsis brda bar bya/_/yang na rte'u zla drug pa/_/bsku dang 'jam rtsi 'bras chan zhu/_/tshad dang ldan pas [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/de bzhin lhag par bya ba dang /_/me stobs skyed pa sbyin par bya/_/kha ra 'o mar sbyar ba la/_/mar 
[\[221a.3\]]ni tshad tsam bcug pa sbyin/_/rte'u lo gnyis lon pa la/_/kha zas sbyin par bya ba yin/_/lug gi sha dang rma bya'i sha/_/khyim bya bya yi sha rnams dang /_/nya dang rus sbal sha rnams las/_/sha khu dang ni kha zas bya/_/sna sman dgongs ka btung ba dang /_/bsku dang 'jam rtsi 
[\[221a.4\]]rtswa yis kyang /_/lus kyi lus gzungs brtas bya ba/_/zla drug bar du bya bar bshad/_/de nas sa 'dir bskyang bya zhing /_/lus dang srog kyang bsrung bar bya/_/sor ni nyi shu bzhi la khru/_/khru bzhi la ni gzhu gang ste/_/gzhu 'dom brgyad stong dag la ni/_/las la sbyor ba'i 
[\[221a.5\]]dpag tshad yin/_/der ni dang por rte'u dag_/rim pa 'di dang ldan pas kyang /_/'dom ni sum brgya nas brtsams te/_/dpag tshad bar du rim gyis bkri/_/dgun zla 'bring dang tha [{]chungs [,]chung [}]tshe/_/de las nyis 'dab bya ba yin/_/dpyid zla ra ba gsum 'gyur te/_/dpyid

[\[221b.1\]]ltar gnas pa yin/_/de ni ldang shing brtse mi nus/_/lci zhing khur gyis non ltar 'gro/_/sbal pa ltar 'gro sbom pa dang /_/lus ni snyoms par skye bar 'gyur/_/sbal pa skrangs par gyur pa ltar/_/brtan par 'gro bar 'gyur mi yin/_/tshil gyis snying ni khyab gyur cing /_/mi nub

[\[221b.7\]]stobs ni phun tshogs pa/_/srog chags thams cad bde ba'i dus/_/dpyid do me tog dri can la/_/phan tshun 'dod pa bskyed pa'i tshe/_/rgod ma 'khrig pa'i don du 'gro/_/der ni mngal du chags gyur pa/_/rta yi nyams mnyam bzang bo yin/_/mngal du chags 
[\[222a\]]
[\[222a.1\]]pa bsrung ba'i phyir/_/nas dang ma sha mar gyis gso/_/dman pa bsten du mi gzhug cing /_/skar khung dra ba can du gzhag_/gom pa dal gyis 'gro bya zhing /_/dben pa'i phyogs su bskyang bar bya/_/khang pa skar khung can der ni/_/bzhi mnyam

[\[222a.4\]]pags srab ba spu snum pa dang /_/'gro legs gzung bar sla ba dang /_/lus kyi yon tan kun ldan zhing /_/mtshan nyid thams cad bsngags pa dang /_/'dzin dang 'jog par byed pa la/_/rtag tu mnyam pa med par dpa'/_/ston ka dag dang zhes 'di bshad/_/rta

[\[222b.1\]]rang bzhin la gnas dang /_/dmar dang de bzhin phra ba ste/_/rnam pa bzhi po gso ba dang /_/bcas par de bzhin bshad pa yin/_/gzhan yang pags pa la sogs pa'i/_/rang bzhin gang yin bshad par bya/_/pags srab mdangs snum phun sum tshogs/_/ba

[\[222b.5\]]rgyas/_/rkan dang ma rkan mig dmar zhing /_/stobs chen gzugs ni bzang bdag_/sha mkhregs pa zhes bya bar bshad/_/gseng khrom me tog mdog 'dra zhing /_/zlum la tshil gyis rgyas shing bzang /_/sha che tshil ni che gyur pa/_/che mkhregs pa yi rta zhes

[\[223a.1\]]de ni rkang mkhregs pa zhes bya/_/snum zhing bska la dri ma med/_/gang zhing ba ra'i 'o ma 'dra/_/gang la de 'dra'i khu ba yod/_/de ni nga yis bshad kyis nyen/_/sra zhing stobs ldan dregs pa bdo/_/rgya cher dga' zhing mgyogs dang ldan/_/rgod ma shin tu tshim

[\[223a.7\]]de bzhin kun byed nus/_/rkang mkhregs par ni gyur ba'i rta/_/stobs dang gzi mdangs phun tshogs 'gyur/_/khu ba mang bar gyur pa'i rta/_/bu mang stobs che 'dod mos med/_/rtsa rgyus mkhregs par gyur ba'i rta/_/gnyid srab ngal ba med par 'gyur/_/snying stobs che ba bdag

[\[224a.1\]]par bya/_/de yi rjes la na tshod brtag_/lus brtags nas ni tshe tshad de/_/ci srid nas ni 'chi ba brtag_/de rjes rin thang gdab bya ste/_/gzhan du bzang po ngan par 'gyur/_/de bzhin rigs dang shes pa dang /_/mtshan nyid bzang dang ngan pa dang /_/mdog

[\[224b.5\]]gsal byas nas/_/rta mi gtong dam mi len nam/_/gang zhig 'ga' bar gyur pa na/_/de la chad pa ci rigs bcad/_/sha li ho tra'i mdo'i gnas las nyo tshong brtag pa'i le'u ste bzhi bcu pa'o/_/gnas dang po rdzogs so//_//sha li ho tra bder gnas la/_/

[\[225a\]]
[\[225a.1\]]sor gnyis chu sbubs bya/_/zlum zhing rtse mo rta rna 'dra/_/'jam zhing shin tu sra ba la/_/bu ga mthe bong shong tsam ni/_/rtse mo'i phyogs su bya ba yin/_/ya so'i gseb tu btang nas ni/_/g.yon phyogs su ni gzhag par bya/_/bde zhing drang por bzhag gnas 
[\[225a.2\]]ni/_/mgo bor slar yang brdeg byas la/_/chu sbubs mtshan nyid de 'dra ba/_/sum cha nye bar bzung nas ni/_/sor bzhir kha yi nang bsrings la/_/gnod pa med par blud par bya/_/ring par sbyin par mi bya zhing /_/nyung nyung yang dang yang mi sbyin/_/de 'dra'i tshul gyi chu 
[\[225a.3\]]sbubs dang /_/rta la btung ba blud par bya/_/'o ma chang las gyur pa'i nang /_/nam yang chu sbubs kyis ni sel/_/de bas chu dang 'o ma ni/_/chu sbubs dang ni blud mi bya/_/chang ni 'bu zhugs rul pa dang /_/skyur zhing mdog nyams spyi bag can/_/gsar dang tshig dang 
[\[225a.4\]]shun pa chags/_/snyigs mar gyur pa smad pa yin/_/gang tshe rta ni skyur ba'am/_/spyi bag can gyi chang blud na/_/de tshe khrag dang mkhris pa ni/_/tha mal pa gnas 'khrug par 'gyur/_/tshig cing nyams pas bad mkhris dang /_/rlung yang skyed par byed pa yin/_/
[\[225a.5\]]dri mi zhim zhing rul pa yis/_/rlung dang bad kan mkhris pa dag_/gnas nas skyod par byed pa dang /_/brgyal dang rmongs bag skyed par 'gyur/_/rnyings dang chang gi snyigs ma dang /_/'bu dang bcas par sbyin na ni/_/rta yi nad gzhi rnams dang ni/_/lus gzungs dag 
[\[225a.6\]]kyang 'khrugs par 'gyur/_/ro dang mthu med chang gzhon dang /_/blud na rlung ni 'khrugs par byed/_/de bas shas cher mngar gyur ba/_/kha dang tsha ba'i ro ldan pa/_/skyur dang lan tshwa cher mi bro/_/bska ba bsngags par gyur pa yin/_/chang ni ro drug rjes 'jug pas/_/yon 
[\[225a.7\]]tan drug dang ldan pa yin/_/mdog bzang dri ni bzang ba dag_/gsal ba rnams ni bsngags pa yin/_/ro tsa rlung ni shad sel zhing /_/dri med stobs kyang 'phel byed yin/_/ngal ba sel zhing yi gar 'gro/_/phyi lo bsil byed yon tan ldan/_/lus kyi nang ni tsha byed 
[\[225b\]]
[\[225b.1\]]cing /_/zhu rjes kyang ni tsha ba yin/_/bad kan smug dang skems pa sel/_/khrag kyang dang bar byed pa yin/_/gcin sri ba dang tshad 'khru ba/_/phan zhing rlung ni bde rgyu byed/_/rtas byed 'jug byed mchog yin zhing /_/mdog kyang gsal bar byed pa yin/_/me drod 
[\[225b.2\]]nyams par zos rjes la/_/chang 'thung me drod skyed par byed/_/rta yi nad ni thams cad la/_/phal cher chang ni phan pa yin/_/chang ni kha ra sbrang rtsi dang /_/sbyar ba btung bar blud gyur na/_/khong pa'i me drod skyed byed cing /_/kha yi ljan ljin sbyong bar byed/_/bu ram yang 
[\[225b.3\]]ni khyad par du/_/ro tsa byed cing rlung mkhris skyed/_/kha zhing bska ba'i ro ldan pa/_/zhu rjes tsha bar gyur pa yin/_/zhu rjes tsha zhing rtsub pas na/_/bad kan sbyong bar byed pa yin/_/de bzhin tsha zhing rtsub pa na/_/mkhris pa rlung ni 'khrugs par byed/_/khar ba sil zhing 
[\[225b.4\]]rtsub bas na/_/drod po min pas rlung skyed byed/_/nim pa seng phrom pri yang ku/_/gla sgang pi pi ling btab dang /_/sbrang rtsi dang ni sbyar ba yi/_/btud pa dpyid kyi dus su bsngags/_/shing mngar ser phrom pi pi ling /_/pri yang ku dang bca' lga btab/_/bu ram dang 
[\[225b.5\]]skyar btung ba blud/_sos ka'i tshe na bsngags pa yin/_/thar shing dang ni bca' lga dang /_/lan tshwa dang ni til mar dang /_/pi pi ling phye btab pa yi/_/rjes su btung ba dbyar tshe bsngags/_/shu ti bca' lga pi pi ling /_/tsi tra ka dang na le sham/_/kha ra rgun 'bru dang 
[\[225b.6\]]sbyar ba'i/_/rjes su btung ba ston tshe bsngags/_/'tsho byed sde tshan pi pi ling /_/shing mngar dang ni ma nu dang /_/seng phrom rgyam tsha dang sbyar ba'i/_/btung ba dgun gyi dus su bsngags/_/zi ra dang ni byi dang ga_/tsha ba gsum dang tsi tra ka_/lce myang tsha dang sbrang rtsi btab/_/
[\[225b.7\]]btung ba dgun gyi dus su bsngags/_/rlung dang mkhris pa bad kan rnams/_/rnam pa du mar 'khrugs gyur pa'i/_/gso ba nye bar bstan pa la/_/rjes su btung ba blud par bya/_/nyi ma shar ma thag tu ni/_/dang po gdong ni bkus nas kyang /_/khong pa dag gyur rta rnams 
[\[226a\]]
[\[226a.1\]]la/_rjes su btung ba blud par bya/_/chu ni grang mo ngag gis kyang /_/gdong du nye bar blugs byas la/_/rtswa ni yid tsam byin nas kyang /_/khrus byas rjes la kha zas sbyin/_/so so'i me stobs so so'i nad/_/so so'i rang bzhin las dpag ste/_/dus ni 
[\[226a.2\]]so sor blud bya bas/_/de phyir so sor btung bar bshad/_/zos pa'i rjes la blud bya bas/_/rjes su btung ba zhes kyang bshad/_/zos pa'i rjes la btung ba ni/_/'bras chang dngas ma 'ba' zhig bsngags/_/skom du chang ni blud gyur pa/_/zas ni yi ga 'byed byed cing /_/

[\[226a.4\]]me drod tshul las ni/_/rdzas su btung ba blud par bya/_/rjes su btung ba lan tshwa ltar/_/bu ram yang ni sbyin par bya/_/pi pi ling dang bca' lga ni/_/der ni srang bzhi gzhug par bya/_/til mar phul gang nyid dang bsre/_/btung ba snga dro blud par bya/_/chang gi nyes pa las gyur 
[\[226a.5\]]pa'i /_/nad ni snang gyur gang yin la/_/gso ba ru ni bshad pa yin/_/sman gyis slar ni bcos par bya/_/rjes su btung ba'i don du ni/_/legs thos kyis ni yang smras pa/_/gang gi dus su gang sbyin zhing /_/tshad dang sman ni gang dang bcas/_/btung ba sman ni gang du sbyin/_/
[\[226a.6\]]chang dngas chang rnying sbyar ba'i chang /_/de bzhin rgun chang la sogs pa/_/gang tshe sbyin pa bdag la gsungs/_/bu yis de skad dris pa dang /_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/dgun che nas chang sbyin bya zhing /_/dgun smad dus su nas chang blud/_/dpyid ni sbrang chang sbyin 
[\[226a.7\]]bya zhing /_sos ka chang dngas sbyin par bya/_/dbyar gyi dus su chang rnying sbyin/_/ston ni rgun chang sbyin par bya/_/zla phyed dus dang sman sbyar ba/_/gang zhing blud bya de bshad bya/_/snga dro khong pa sbyangs pa la/_/chang gi dngas ma phul gang dang /_/de bzhin til mar 
[\[226b\]]
[\[226b.1\]]phul phyed dang /_/kha ra dkar po srang bcu dang /_/tsha ba gsum ni srang gcig dang /_/lan tshwa yang ni srang gcig dag_/rta ni chen po la sbyin bya/_/de yis de la thob par 'gyur/_/bar ma la ni brgyad cha dag_/de la rnam par dbye bar bya/_/pi pi ling dang na le sham/_/

[\[226b.3\]]de yongs su spang bar bya/_/de bzhin 'o ma blud rjes la/_/chang sbyin par yang mi bya'o/_/chang 'thungs rjes la skom blud pa'am/_/zas kyi rjes la blud pa'ang rung /_/zas ni dus rnams kun du sbyin/_/skom ni phyi dro blud par bya/_/skom blud rjes la rtsol drag pa/_/rnam 
[\[226b.4\]]pa kun du spang bar bya/_/zla phyed dus su gang sbyin pa/_/de ni ci bzhin sngar bshad yin/_/sha li ho tra'i gnas bdun pa las rjes su btung ba bstan pa'i le'u'o//_//sha li ho tra thub mchog la/_/legs thos kyis ni yang dris pa/_/sman pa mchog gis rta 
[\[226b.5\]]rnams la/_/ro drug po ni dgos par gsungs/_/ro drug po ni dgos pa la/_/'di la shal cher lan tshwa ni/_/sbyor ba kun la rab shes te/_/lus ni rtas par byed pa dang /_/kha zas len par nus pa dang /_/lto bar phyin par gyur pa ni/_/legs nyes ci 'gyur rgyu de gsungs/_/
[\[226b.6\]]de skad dris tshe legs ldan ni/_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/rta ni rlung gi shugs ldan zhing /_/rlung shas lhag par che ba yin/_/rlung 'joms pas ni ro drug las/_/lan tshwa rta la mchog tu bsngags/_/mdangs ni rnam par 'phel byed cing /_/sbyor ba kun dang rjes mthun la/_/
[\[226b.7\]]ljang ba shas cher mngar bas na/_/'ju dka' bar ni bshad pa yin/_/de bsal na tshwa phan gyur cing /_/rta rnams rtas par byed pa yin/_/gzhan yang lan tshwa'i yon tan rnams/_/nga yis bshad kyis de nyan gyis/_/'drul zhing drod ni skyed byed la/_/zas 'ju lus rgyas 
[\[227a\]]
[\[227a.1\]]byed pa'i mchog_/drags na bad kan skyed byed cing /_/lus kyang ltod par 'gyur ba bshad/_/ran pas kha ni sbyong byed cing /_/stobs skyed mdog ni dag byed yin/_/gang zhig rlung nad kyis nyen dang /_/gang zhig rtswa dang 'gras pa dang /_/gang yang 
[\[227a.2\]]yid mi bder gyur dang /_/gnyin ni med par gyur ba dang /_/gang zhig me drod nyams pa'am/_/gang zhig me drod ma snyoms dang /_/lus gzungs rnams la gnod bya las/_/brtas kyang tshon por ma gyur dang /_/de la tshad ldan lan tshwa ni/_/byin na nad rnams sel byed yin/_/

[\[227a.4\]]bstan/_/rgyam tsha dang ni kha ru tsha/_/rgya mtsho'i tsha dang mtsho tsha dang /_/ya bak+Sha ra sro h+ni ka_/rtsal ru tsha ni bdun pa yin/_/bul tog brgyad pa nyid yin no/_/'di ni lan tshwa'i tshogs su 'dod/_/kun la rgya tsha mchog yin te/_de nas rim gyis yon tan chung /_/
[\[227a.5\]]'di rnams sbyor ba'i tshad dang ni/_/dus kyang nga las nyan par gyis/_/zas dang rjes su btung ba la/_/de yi khu ba dang sbyar bya/_/snga dro'am yang na phyi dro'am/_/yang na khrus byas dus su ni/_/rtag tu lan tshwa byin gyur na/_/me drod rnams par 'phel byed yin/_/

[\[228a.1\]]las lan tshwa'i skyon dang yon tan bstan pa'i le'u'o//_//legs thos thos pa phun tshogs pas/_/sha li ho tra la dris pa/_/khyed ni rta yi tshe rig byed/_/yan lag brgyad kyi de nyid mkhyen/_/rgyu dang mi rgyu'i 'dri ba ni/_/chu la dag ni las pa lags/_/

[\[270b.6\]]'gyur zhing /_/pho ba'i nad rnams 'byung mi 'gyur/_/zos pa myur du 'ju 'gyur zhing /_/bde ba kho nar sbyin par byed/_/lan tshwas rta yi gcin tu dag_/bde ba kho nar 'byung bar 'gyur/_/bde ba kho nar 'byung bar 'gyur/_/lan tshwa rta yi tshe yin pas/_/dus ni rtag tu bsngags pa yin/_/sman

[\[271a\]]
[\[271a.1\]]tshe na lan tshwa ni/_/nim pa'i lo ma dang sbyar sbyin/_/dgun gyi tshe ni dgu thi yi/_/lo ma dang bcas sbyin par bya/_/pa to la yi lo mar bcas/_/dgun smad dang ni dpyid dus sbyin/_/sos ka dang ni ston gyi tshe/_/mar dang sbyar ba sbyin 
[\[271a.2\]]par bya/_/lan tshwa yi ni sbyor ba 'di/_/bu la nga yis shes pa yin/_/sha li ho tra'i gsang ba'i gnas te brgyad pa las lan tshwa'i cho ga'o//_//tshangs ba drag po khyab 'jug ni/_/bad kan mkhris pa rlung dag cad/_/phan tshun bsten par byed pa 
[\[271a.3\]]dang /_/gcig la gcig ni ma 'dres gnas/_/de la tshangs pa bad kan te/_/drag po mkhris pa yin par bshad/_/khyab 'jug rlung gi bdag nyid can/_/de ltar tshul ni rnam gsum yin/_/sa la bcos mas gnas pa yin/_/tshangs pa la sogs lha 
[\[271a.4\]]gsum pa'o/_/lus can rnams kyi brten pa'i rgyu/_/a ru ra dang skyu ru ra/_/ba ru ra ste 'bras bu gsum/_/'di yi yon tan rnams dang ni/_/sbyor ba'i khyad par bshad par bya/_/rked pa brla yi nad sel cing /_/lus zungs thams cad dang bar byed/_/mdog

[\[271a.6\]]yis/_/nad ni sbyor bar byed pa yin/_/a ru ra ni cha gcig ste/_/ba ru ra ni de'i nyis 'gyur/_/skyu ru ra ni ba ru ra'i/_/nyis 'gyur kho nar gzhug par bya/_/sman ni bre gang dag dang ni/_/chu ni bre bzhi bskol bar bya/_/bzhi cha lus par gyur pa dang /_/

[\[271b.1\]]sre tres dang /_/pi pi ling dang sbyar ba blud/_/mig gi nad dang rlig skrangs dang /_/rma dang rgyus nad 'khums pa dang /_/rdo dang g.yan ba 'brum phran dang /_/'brum bu lto yi nad rnams dang /_/de bzhin lto nad gzhong 'dra dang /_/lkog nad 'bras kyi phreng ba la/_/zhag

[\[271b.3\]]mchis/_/skye gnas rnam pa du zhes bgyi/_/ming dang kha dog mtshan nyid dang /_/rta yi nyes pa gang dang gang /_/sel 'gyur thub mchog khyed kyis gsungs/_/bu yis de skad dris pa dang /_/sha li ho tras slar smras pa/_/brgya byin bdud rtsi 'thungs ba na/_/thigs

[\[271b.5\]]ba dang /_/kha dang skyur bar grags dang ldan/_/de ltar ro lnga bshad nas ni/_/de yi ming ni bshad par bya/_/rnam rgyal 'phel ba zhes bya dang /_/skem byed de bzhin bdud rtsi dang /_/nag po dang ni 'jigs med dang /_/'tsho byed ces ni ming du grags/_/rnam rgyal ku 
[\[271b.6\]]ba lta bur zlum/_/'phel byed shin tu zlum pa yin/_/skem byed rus pa cha zhing dkar/_/bdud rtsi rtse mo sbom pa yin/_/'tsho byed gser gyi mdog can de/_/'jig med zur ni lnga ba yin/_/nag po zur gsum ba nyid de/_/rigs ni bdun du shes 
[\[271b.7\]]par bya/_/'jig med rta yi rkang ba yi/_/nad la rtag tu gtang par bya/_/'phel byed dang ni bdud rtsi dag_/rtag tu rta yi lus la gtang /_/nag po sbyong bar byed par bshad/_/'tsho byed stobs ni 'phel bar byed/_/skem byed mig gi nad la gtang /_/

[\[272a.1\]]rnam par rgyal bas 'khru bar byed/_/de ltar rigs dang ming dag gis/_/rnam pa bdun du bshad pa yin/_/rlung dang mkhris pa las gyur dang /_/khyad par du ni bad kan nad/_/mngon du 'phrog byed a ru ra/_/skyur bas rlung ni sel bar byed/_/mngar

[\[272b.1\]]ni ma lus 'joms par byed/_/sha li ho tra'i gsang ba'i gnas te brgyad pa las a ru ra'i cho ga'o//_//sha li ho tra thub mchog la/_/legs thos kyis ni yang dris pa/_/gu gul ji ltar byung gyur cing /_/rang bzhin mtshan nyid ci 'dra lags/_/tshad dang

[\[272b.7\]]bya/_/sha ni de la sbyin par bya/_/chu ni nye bar bskol ba dang /_/ljang pa skam po sbyin pa bsngags/_/rta ni chen po nyid la ni/_/mthar gyis drug cu rtsa bzhi sbyin/_/bar ma la ni bzhi bcu brgyad/_/nya bar sbyin pa nyid du bya/_/chung ngu la ni srang sum cu/_/rtsa gnyis 
[\[273a\]]
[\[273a.1\]]kyi ni tshad du sbyin/_/'di ni rta yi nad gzhi gsum/_/'khrugs par gyur la bsngags pa yin/_/tshigs skrangs pa dang skran dang ni/_/de bzhin rma yi nad rnams dang /_/lus rengs yan lag skrangs pa dang /_/skrangs dang gzhang kha'i nad

[\[273b\]]
[\[273b.1\]]snum zhing brtan la ro lngar ldan/_/tsha zhing stobs ni skyed par byed/_/lci zhing ro tsha byed pa ste/_/skyur ba'i ro dang dral ba yin/_/de bas ro ma tshang ba zhes/_/drang srong rnams kyis bshad pa yin/_/gang zhig rlung gi shugs kyis ni/_/yan lag 'byung ngam 'khums gyur

[\[273b.6\]]tan bshad par bya/_/rlung gi nad la mar dang ni/_/sha khu dang sbyar gtang bar bya/_/mkhris pa can la 'o ma dang /_/legs par bskol te sbyin par bya/_/rang skyur kany+ji dang sbyar bas/_/sbo dang bshang ba 'gags pa sel/_/khong ba'i srin bu rnams la ni/_/ba

[\[274a.1\]]sel zhing /_/nad kun 'joms par byed pa yin/_/til mar dang sbyar bad rlung 'joms/_/mar dang sbyar bas rlung mkhris sel/_/'o ma bu ram shing khur sbyar/_/khrag dang mkhris pa sel byed yin/_/me drod nyams pa rgyam tsha dang /_/chang dang sbyar te sbyin par

[\[274a.3\]]kyis ni nyen pa dang /_/tshigs su byung ba'i sman mchog yin/_/sgog pa las ni snum nyis 'gyur/_/bya ba sbyor ba la rab bsngags/_/snod ni bzang po'i nad bcug ste/_/shin tu dben pa'i gnas su sba/_/lo gcig lon par gyur pa'i tshe/_/dri ni gang tshe byung gyur pa/_/de tshe

[\[274a.5\]]bshad/_/rta yi mchog ni rtag tu ni/_/de la srang ni nyi shu sbyin/_/bar ma la ni bco lnga ste/_/tha ma la ni bcu nyid sbyin/_/phag dang de bzhin ma he dang /_/yang nya byi'u gong ma sreg_/rma bya dang ni [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sha/_/mar btab g.yos ni legs par byas/_/kha zas bzang po

[\[274b\]]
[\[274b.1\]]tra'i gsang bu'i gnas te brgyad pa las sgog skya'i cho ga rdzogs so//_//sha li ho tra drang srong mchog_/nyi ma dang ni gzi mnyam pa/_/rta khang na ni 'dug pa na/_/legs thos kyis ni yang dris pa/_/rta la go cha bskon pa ni/_/ci 'dra ba zhig bgyi ba gsungs/_/de bzhin yan lag

[\[274b.3\]]ba yi/_/skyes bu sngar ni smras pa yin/_/sum cu ma len bar ma yin/_/lnga po 'bri ba chung ngu yin/_/grags ldan rnams kyi mi yi tshad/_/de ltar sngar ni bshad pa yin/_/shing rta pa yang rta yi ni/_/bstan bcos shes shing legs par byas/_/mda' gzhu ral gri la

[\[275a\]]
[\[275a.1\]]de bzhin thag pa yi/_/tshad ni 'dir ni des ma byas/_/mdung la'ang tshad gcig yod gyur pas/_/'di 'dra gdags zhes de la smras/_/rta yi rgyab tu dgab bya ba/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]ri'u bal shar dag_/spu gzhan 'jam pa las byas pa/_/sra zhing shin tu 'jam 
[\[275a.2\]]pa bya/_/shin tu legs par btags pa las/_/sna tshogs mdog gis brgyan par bya/_/de yi 'brel thag dang bcas pa/_/srang ni sum cu'i tshad du bya/_/gdong rgyan srang ni nyi shu lnga/_/legs par ltas pa gdags par bya/_/glo ni sdom par byed pas na/_/rgyab tu gnod 
[\[275a.3\]]skyed mi 'gyur bar/_/lug bal shin tu 'jam pa yis/_/srad bu las ni legs par bya/_/yang na pags pa legs nyed pa'am/_/ras bal dkar po las byas pa'am/_/dar ram gso ma dar la byas/_/gzhan yang mdzes shing sra la 'jam/_/stan dang cha byad legs 
[\[275a.4\]]pa yi/_/rdzas kyi bzo ni du ma bya/_/de bzhin gdong gzan sang tshad ni/_/bcu dag rna ba'i gra nas gdags/_/gdong ni legs par srung byed pa/_/shin tu legs pa nyid du bya/_/g.yer kha shin tu snyan pa ni/_/srang re yi ni tshad byas pas/_/sra zhing lus ni brtan pa

[\[275a.6\]]pa'o/_//de nas shing rta dang sbyar ba'i/_/nges pa nga yis bshad par bya/_/rta ni gang gi mchu ltag na/_/shin tu shis pa'i mtshan ma yod/_/ba dan bzang po'am zla ba rgyas/_/nya dang rgyal mtshan yod gyur pa/_/rta de rtag tu bsngags pa yin/_/de 'dra'i mtshan nyid dang

[\[275b.6\]]gnas na 'phel/_/me yi bu nyid khyod dran byos/_/ko tu ba li nor bu'ang dran/_/'o ma'i mtshe las bdud rtsi'i phyir/_/lha dang lha min gyis bsrubs tshe/_/dang po u tsa sra ba byung /_/de las khyod skyes dran par gyis/_/shugs mchog ston de nga yis ni/_/dgra dpung ma lus las las rgyal gyis/_/

[\[276a.1\]]gal de log par 'gro 'gyur na/_/bram ze pha ma gsod 'dra yi/_/nyes de khyod kyis 'thob par 'gyur/_/gal te yul las rgyal gyur na/_/bdag po bde ba nyid skyed byed/_/oM na ma/_/Adid t+ya ya u ba ya ga ga ga swAhA/_/rta yi rna ba g.yas par ni/_/'thab pa'i tshe na brjod

[\[276a.4\]]tsaib su t+ya s+ya/_mA ni nam ta pa sat+ta/_/b+ha dra saM b+ha ma tsa r+ye na/_/pa b+ha nas pa len tsa sra b+ha bA n+tu ta/_/shwA sA bar+d+ha sa twaM tu raM ga maH_oM ya ya ya ya/_gag gag swA hA/_/'di ni bdag nyid chen po ni/_/rta yi snying por gyur pa yin/_/g.yul gyi dus su dran bya yi/_/gzhan du nam yang ma 
[\[276a.5\]]yin no/_/legs par btags shing go bgos la/_/'dra ba la rta gzhon nas ni/_/'gal zhig rang gi don gyi phyir/_/rmongs pas bdag nyid skyob byed kyi/_/legs thos rta yi lus yan lag_/legs par srung ba mi byed na/_/de tshe sdig can dmyal bar ni/_/yun ring du ni

[\[276b.1\]]rta'i tshe'i rig byang mtha' dag rjes su bstan pa zhes bya ba rgyas pa rdzogs so//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po A tsA r+ya d+ha rma shrI b+ha dra dang /_/bu d+g+ta shrI shAn+ti dang /_/zhu chen gyi lo tsA ba chen po dge slong rin chen bzang pos bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa'o//_//dpal ldan rgyal po'i

[\[276b.3\]]khyed la mkhas dang rgyal phran gzhan/_/bram ze slong mo ba rnams kyis/_/rang re gser gyi dbang phyug tu/_/rgyal chen khyed ni gcig tu bsgrags/_/mi dbang rgyal po dgyes pa dang /_/gsang dpyad kyis gzhan phan gdags phyir/_/bka' yis bskul bas gzhung rgyas 'di/_/bod kyi

[\[277a.1\]]brda sprod sgra bsgyur lo tsA bas/_/lha mi skal ba can gyi mchod pa'i gnas/_/rin chen kun la yod kyang bzang po med/_/rin chen sngon 'gro bzang pos mtha' brgyan pa/_/gus pa sngon 'gro'i bka' yis rab bskul bas/_/

[\[277a.3\]]grags pa'i 'od zer bye ba rab spro zhing /_/rgyal mtshan thub bstan shar ri'i rtser son nas/_/tshogs gnyis rta ljang shugs kyis gling bzhi yi/_/mun sel nam mkha'i nor bu de la 'dud/_/khyed kyis bskal ba bye ba dpag yas su/_/rang gzhan don du
